Smoke-tested four paths: non-existent path → exit 2 with "path: ref points to a non-existent file/directory"; directory without launcher → "directory does not contain a recognizable launcher"; missing version (no `version.txt` + no `--runtime-version`) → "path: ref lacks a runtime version"; happy path with sibling `version.txt` → resolver picks it up and proceeds to idlc invocation. **Item C — DEFERRED**: reading `circeVersion` + `scalaVersion` from an sbt-emitted `runtime.properties` manifest requires either a new sbt task in `build.sbt` (constraint #3 prohibits this) or a parser that reads `project/Deps.scala` from inside the harness (Deps.scala is sbtgen-generated and not parse-friendly without instantiation). The `ScalaAdapter.CirceVersion = "0.14.14"` pin remains; cited in README troubleshooting + known follow-ups. **Item D — Operator README**: completely rewrote `regression-harness/README.md` (163 → ~290 LOC). New sections: **Quickstart** (3 invocation examples — sanity, IMPL-9 vs HEAD, custom git refs); **Decision tree** (when to use `self` vs `git:<ref>` vs `path:<launcher>` with cost column); **LLM workflow** (manual prompt invocation, `claude` CLI one-liner, Anthropic API curl example, determinism contract); **Per-sha cache layout** + cleanup commands; **Exit codes** (table); **Troubleshooting** (per-language sections capturing the M1-M5 surprises: sbt-native-packager short-flag eating; scala-cli `using repository` (was `using resolver`); `tsx` necessity over Node strip-types; ESM extensionless-imports issue; csproj `EnableDefaultCompileItems=false`; IRT/Transport + UrlEscaper.cs exclusion; Newtonsoft.Json 13.0.3 pin; `++ 2.13.18` cross-build sequencing); **Stale agent worktrees** (`git worktree prune` hygiene step + `git worktree remove --force`); **Recovering from failed `git worktree add`** (the 3-step defensive sequence in `addWorktree`); **CI integration sketch** (GitHub Actions YAML — out of scope to land but the doc helps the user wire it up); **Known follow-ups** (sbt manifest, auto-LLM, lint pass, full corpus, CI, cross-machine cache portability). **Item E — Worktree cleanup verification**: the M2 design (copy `stage/` tree to per-sha cache, then remove the `git worktree`) is correct — the launcher's bash wrapper resolves `lib/*.jar` paths via `dirname $0 → ..`, so as long as the full stage tree (bin/ + lib/) survives, the launcher runs after worktree removal. Verified empirically by impl9-vs-head-{scala,ts,cs} (all using the cached `ea697f5c0692/stage/bin/idealingua-v1-compiler` launcher; the original worktree under that cache dir was removed by `removeWorktree` after the initial M2 build). Stale `.claude/worktrees/agent-*` entries hygiene step documented in README troubleshooting. **Item F — Improved error messages**: replaced terse FATAL lines and bare `throw new RuntimeException(...)` messages with the structured `Expected / Observed / Next` pattern at all five resolver throw sites (sbt stage failure, sbt stage missing launcher, self publishLocal failure, git[sha] publishM2 failure, git[sha] publishM2 missing artifacts) plus the three path:-resolver paths (missing file, directory without launcher, missing version). Harness orchestration sites (`runIdlc` failure, `adapter.buildAndRun` failure, `Canonicalize.canonicalize` failure) split each FATAL into four labeled lines for legibility. **Item G — `matrix` selftest mode**: `selftest.sh matrix` runs all six cells (`sanity-scala`, `impl9-vs-head-scala`, `sanity-typescript`, `impl9-vs-head-typescript`, `sanity-csharp`, `impl9-vs-head-csharp`) sequentially, captures per-cell exit codes, and prints a labeled PASS/FAIL summary table at the end. Exit 0 iff all six pass. Also added long-form aliases (`sanity-scala`, `impl9-vs-head-typescript`, …) so the cells share a uniform name scheme with the matrix summary. **Item H — `--dry-run` mode**: added `--dry-run` flag that prints the planned resolver calls, idlc invocations, sample-app cache lookup, adapter materialization plan, canonicalization step, and diff/report output dir, then exits 0 without executing anything. Useful for confirming flag handling + scratch directory layout before kicking off a long build. **Verification**: (i) `./regression-harness/selftest.sh matrix` → 6/6 PASS (zero divergences across 3 keys per cell, total 18 wire-line comparisons). The matrix exercises self-vs-self, git-vs-self, all three languages, the cache-hit fast path for `ea697f5`, and the publishM2 short-circuit for TS / C# sides. (ii) FROZEN harness contracts (`regenerateGoldens`, `verifyGoldens`, `runWireFixtures`, `runCrossLangInterop`) × {Scala 2.13.18, Scala 3.8.3} = 8 cells: **structurally preserved by isolation** — every M6 edit is confined to `regression-harness/`; the FROZEN harness module under `idealingua-v1-test-harness/` is byte-unchanged from M5. (iii) `path:<launcher>` smoke: failure paths exercised end-to-end with `/nonexistent`, `/tmp` (dir without launcher), and a fake stage with no `version.txt`; happy path exercised with a fake stage carrying `version.txt=1.2.3-TEST` and via `--runtime-version 9.9.9`. (iv) publishM2 short-circuit: `sanity-ts` log line confirms `skipping Scala-runtime publishLocal (self, target lang is not scala; version=1.4.20-SNAPSHOT)`. **LOC totals (M6 additions)**: `IdlcResolver.scala` 312 → 470 LOC (+158: resolvePath, needsScalaRuntime threading, structured errors), `Harness.scala` 286 → 340 LOC (+54: runtimeVersion + dryRun args, structured FATAL messages), `selftest.sh` 114 → 156 LOC (+42: matrix mode + 6 alias cases), `README.md` 163 → ~290 LOC (+127 net). Total ~380 LOC net added, well under the 2000-LOC budget. **Surprises**: (1) **The session's gitStatus header showed a stale HEAD** (`fa11569 PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fe`) that did not exist on this worktree's branch (which was at the orphaned tag-bump commit `40c5f64`). The actual M5 commit `615a6bd` lived on `wip/necromancy`; needed `git reset --hard 615a6bd` to land on the right tree before any M6 work could begin. (2) **`detectRepoRoot()` walks past worktrees** because it requires `.git` to be a *directory*, not a file (worktree `.git` is a gitdir pointer file). The cache + scratch land in the parent repo (`/home/pavel/work/safe/idealingua-v1/target/`) regardless of which worktree the harness is invoked from — works correctly because all worktrees share the same `.git/`. Documented in README; no fix needed. (3) **The parent repo's `target/regression-harness/cache/idlc/ea697f5c0692/` was already populated** from a prior M5 run; this short-circuited the impl9-vs-head cells (cache hit, no sbt rebuild) and made the verification runtime <2 min instead of ~20 min cold. (4) **`bash` shell aliases for `grep` and `find` are broken** in this environment (`-G: error while loading shared libraries`); had to fall back to `command find` / `command grep`. **F-followups (M6)**: F-M6-1 — read `circeVersion` + `scalaVersion` from an sbt-emitted manifest at publishM2 time (Item C deferral); needs a new sbt task or a sbtgen extension. F-M6-2 — automatic LLM invocation behind `--llm-cli claude|codex|none` (defer): the prompt format is stable, the wiring is straightforward but out of M6 scope per the brief. F-M6-3 — full main-tests-corpus sample app per language (M6+ planned per plan §11). F-M6-4 — CI integration: lift the GitHub Actions sketch in README into a real workflow. Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) `<noreply@anthropic.com>`.

- **regression-harness M5: C# adapter** (2026-05-12) — Fifth milestone of the wire-format regression harness; closes plan §11 M5. Adds `CsharpAdapter` (145 LOC) implementing the `LangAdapter` trait for `--lang csharp`, paired with `templates/csharp/Driver.csproj`, prompt `prompts/sample-app-csharp.md`, and a hand-rolled 3-fixture sample app + meta sidecar under `selftest-corpus/dtofields-only/.idl-regression/sample_app.cs{,.meta}` matching the Scala/TS samples byte-for-byte. **Per-side build flow**: invoke the resolved launcher with `:csharp --define=layout=PLAIN --disable-zip` (no role-local flags needed; `withRuntime=true` is the default — `LP.noRuntime` is opt-out, so the IRT runtime tree is always emitted into the per-side `<target>/csharp/IRT/`). Adapter copies the generated `csharp/` subtree verbatim into `workDir/csharp/` (no skip-filter needed — idlc PLAIN layout for C# emits only `IRT/` + user-domain dirs, no `.csproj` or solution files); then materializes our own `Driver.csproj` at `workDir/Driver.csproj`. **csproj design**: pins `<TargetFramework>net9.0</TargetFramework>` + `Newtonsoft.Json` 13.0.3 (matching FROZEN test-harness `idealingua-v1-test-harness/src/main/csharp/Driver.csproj`), sets `<EnableDefaultCompileItems>false</EnableDefaultCompileItems>` (otherwise SDK auto-globs collide with our explicit `<Compile Include="csharp/**/*.cs" ...>` items and produce NETSDK1022 errors). **IRT include strategy** mirrors the FROZEN test-harness's exclusion pattern: includes `csharp/IRT/Marshaller/**`, `csharp/IRT/Logger/**`, plus the 7 root IRT files we need (Dispatcher, RTTI, Either, Void, ILogger, IServiceDispatcher, DispatcherException); EXCLUDES `csharp/IRT/Transport/**` (pulls `WebSocketSharp` NuGet — irrelevant for stdout-emitting sample apps) and EXCLUDES `csharp/IRT/UrlEscaper.cs` (depends on `System.Web` which is NOT auto-referenced under net9.0 SDK; would need an explicit `<Reference Include="System.Web" />` like the FROZEN harness uses, but we don't need URL escaping here). The user-domain tree is included via `<Compile Include="csharp/**/*.cs" Exclude="csharp/IRT/**/*.cs" />`. **Per-sha runtime cache**: NOT needed. idlc `:csharp` with `withRuntime=true` inlines the IRT tree into the generated output (same self-contained pattern as TS). The C# IRT runtime has NO NuGet feed dependency (only Newtonsoft.Json which we pin in the csproj template) so there is no per-sha m2/NuGet repo to materialize. `IdlcResolution.runtimeVersion` is recorded as an XML comment in the rendered csproj for human-readability. Same future-optimization carve-out as TS: the M2 per-sha Scala `publishM2` step is currently still performed for git refs even when `--lang csharp` is requested — deferred to M6 polish. **Build+run**: `dotnet run --project <workDir> --verbosity quiet --nologo` with a 5-minute timeout. stdout captures the canonical NDJSON; stderr goes to a sibling `.stderr` file for diagnostics. **Wire format**: an `IntPairStruct` serialized via `JsonConvert.SerializeObject(v, S)` invokes `IntPairStruct_JsonNetConverter` (auto-generated under `Idltest/Dtofields/IntPair.cs`) which writes `{"x":0,"y":0}` — concrete struct emits its fields directly, no polymorphic wrapper. Note the C#/Scala name-casing asymmetry: C# namespaces are PascalCase (`Idltest.Dtofields.IntPairStruct`) but the on-wire `wireId` stays dotted-lowercase via `RTTI_FULLCLASSNAME` const (`idltest.dtofields.IntPair.Struct`). **Acceptance test (passed end-to-end on `wip/necromancy`)**: `./regression-harness/idl-regress --project regression-harness/selftest-corpus/dtofields-only --old git:ea697f5 --new self --lang csharp` → exit 0 with zero divergences across 3 keys. Generated C# trees from IMPL-9 (ea697f5) and HEAD are byte-identical (`diff -rq /tmp/csharp-{probe,probe-impl9}` empty). **Wire output verified byte-identical between IMPL-9 (ea697f5) and HEAD on C# backend**: `idltest.dtofields.IntPair.Struct/zero {"x":0,"y":0}`, `IntPair.Struct/mixed {"x":17,"y":-3}`, `WHPair.Struct/default {"h":480,"w":640}` — same canonical NDJSON as Scala + TS adapters produce (Canonicalize re-sorts keys; C#'s `Newtonsoft.Json` emits in source-declared order `{"w":640,"h":480}` which Canonicalize normalises to `{"h":480,"w":640}`). **Harness wiring**: `SupportedLangs` set extended to include `"csharp"`; adapter dispatch in `Harness.run` and idlc role mapping in `Harness.runIdlc` each grow a `csharp` case. Harness's `//> using file` directive list extended with `adapters/CsharpAdapter.scala`. The `LangAdapter` trait is unchanged. The C# adapter reads only `IdlcResolution.runtimeVersion` (for csproj comment substitution) and ignores `runtimeRepoUri`/`runtimeRepoPath`. **`srcExt` map** extended with `csharp → cs` so the on-disk file is `sample_app.cs` (correct extension for IDE / editor language detection / msbuild) while the meta sidecar remains `sample_app.cs.meta`. **selftest.sh extended** with `sanity-cs` (HEAD-vs-HEAD C#) + `impl9-vs-head-cs` (git:ea697f5 vs HEAD C#) modes; `selftest-expectations/impl9-vs-head.csharp.json` committed as the baseline (initially empty per plan §9.2 — non-empty diff is a real audit signal). **dotnet availability**: `dotnet` is on PATH via the project's nix-shell at `/nix/store/.../dotnet-sdk-wrapped-9.0.307/bin/dotnet`, reporting `9.0.307`. The csproj template targets `net9.0` which the SDK provides directly. **Did NOT short-circuit publishM2 for non-Scala langs**: investigated the suggestion from the M4 task entry — `Harness.run` currently always materializes both sides' `IdlcResolution` via `IdlcResolver.resolve` which unconditionally runs the Scala publishM2 step for `git:` refs (per `IdlcResolver.buildGit`). Short-circuiting requires plumbing the `lang` value into `IdlcResolver.resolve` and conditionalising the publishM2 task list — a non-trivial signature change that touches the shared resolver. Deferred to M6 polish so M5 stays a tight C# milestone. **Investigation findings**: (1) **IDLCArgs has NO `enableNUnit` flag** — the M5 plan reference mentioned it as a possible role-local define, but `IDLCArgs.scala`'s `LP ParserDef` exposes only `target`/`manifest`/`credentials`/`disable-runtime`/`disable-zip`/`define`. The C# manifest carries an `enableNUnit` boolean but it's set via the manifest JSON (default `false`), not via `-d enableNUnit=…`. No flag needed for our adapter — `:csharp --define=layout=PLAIN --disable-zip` is exactly the same shape as the TS adapter, role-name swapped. (2) **`/** ... **/` Scaladoc + `**/*.cs` glob collision**: my first draft of `CsharpAdapter.scala` had a scaladoc comment that quoted the csproj globs `IRT/Marshaller/**`, `IRT/Transport/**`, etc. — Scala 3.8 reads `**/` as `*` followed by `*/` (end-of-comment) which terminates the comment block mid-paragraph and the next line fails to parse as Scala. Reworded the comment to use `IRT/Marshaller, IRT/Logger (recursive)` instead of the literal glob. (3) **`Newtonsoft.Json.JsonConvert.SerializeObject` returns field order from the generated converter**: `IntPairStruct_JsonNetConverter.WriteJson` calls `writer.WritePropertyName("x"); ...` in source order, so output is `{"x":0,"y":0}` for `IntPair` and `{"w":640,"h":480}` for `WHPair`. This matches the TS adapter exactly. Canonicalize.canonicalize re-sorts keys so the wire NDJSON is `{"h":480,"w":640}` across all three adapters. (4) **`EnableDefaultCompileItems=false` is required** — otherwise dotnet emits NETSDK1022 "Duplicate 'Compile' items" for every file we list explicitly (the default glob `**/*.cs` and our explicit globs both hit the same files). (5) **`<Reference Include="System.Web" />` not needed** — only `IRT/UrlEscaper.cs` uses `System.Web.HttpUtility`; excluding that single file lets the build complete without pulling the `System.Web.Services` NuGet adapter or the legacy mono-style assembly reference. (6) **IMPL-9 idlc generates IDENTICAL C# to HEAD for dtofields-only** — `diff -rq` between the two generated trees yields no output, confirming the typer modernization is wire-strict on the C# backend for this corpus. (7) **Worktree-vs-main-repo `detectRepoRoot()` subtlety**: agent runs in a `git worktree` where `.git` is a file, not a directory, so `detectRepoRoot()` walks past the worktree root and lands on the main repo (`/home/pavel/work/safe/idealingua-v1`). To run the acceptance test I had to rsync the new harness files into the main repo first (then revert after testing). The committed code path remains correct because at the time of merge into `wip/necromancy` the files live directly in the repo root. **LOC totals (M5 additions)**: 6 NEW files, 320 LOC: `adapters/CsharpAdapter.scala` (145) / `templates/csharp/Driver.csproj` (32) / `prompts/sample-app-csharp.md` (103) / `selftest-corpus/dtofields-only/.idl-regression/sample_app.cs` (32) / `selftest-corpus/dtofields-only/.idl-regression/sample_app.cs.meta` (1) / `selftest-expectations/impl9-vs-head.csharp.json` (7). Edits: `Harness.scala` (+5 lines: csharp `SupportedLangs` entry, adapter dispatch, idlc role mapping, `//> using file` directive), `SampleAppGen.scala` (+1 line: `csharp → cs` in `srcExt`), `selftest.sh` (+31 lines: `sanity-cs` + `impl9-vs-head-cs` modes + mode-list update), `README.md` (+49/-25 lines: status table M5 column + C# requirements + file table + roadmap retarget to M6). Well under the plan's 1500 LOC guidance. **No changes to FROZEN harness, build.sbt, or sbt modules**. Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) `<noreply@anthropic.com>`. **Path to M6 (polish + docs)**: (a) short-circuit the M2 Scala `publishM2` step when `--lang` ∈ {typescript, csharp} (plumb `lang` into `IdlcResolver.resolve`, conditionalise the publishM2 task list — modest signature change but cuts ~30 s off the first `git:`-ref invocation for non-Scala adapters); (b) lift `--regen-sample-app` into an optional auto-invocation of a configured LLM CLI (`--llm-cli claude|codex|…`); (c) `path:<launcher>` form in `IdlcResolver` (pre-built launcher, skips sbt entirely); (d) full main-tests-corpus sample app per language (auto-generated, not hand-rolled); (e) read `CirceVersion` from a manifest emitted by sbt rather than hard-coding in `ScalaAdapter`; (f) end-to-end docs page under `docs/` once M6 stabilises the operator workflow.
- **regression-harness M4: TypeScript adapter** (2026-05-13) — Fourth milestone of the wire-format regression harness; closes plan §11 M4. Adds `TypescriptAdapter` (186 LOC) implementing the `LangAdapter` trait for `--lang typescript`, paired with `templates/typescript/{package.json, tsconfig.json}`, prompt `prompts/sample-app-typescript.md`, and a hand-rolled 3-fixture sample app + meta sidecar under `selftest-corpus/dtofields-only/.idl-regression/sample_app.ts{,.meta}` matching the Scala sample byte-for-byte. **Per-side build flow**: invoke the resolved launcher with `:typescript --define=layout=PLAIN --disable-zip` (no role-local flags needed; `withRuntime=true` is the default — `LP.noRuntime` is opt-out, so the IRT runtime tree is always emitted into the per-side `<target>/typescript/irt/`). Adapter copies the generated `typescript/` subtree into `workDir/`, **filtering out** the `package.json` / `tsconfig.json` / `tsconfig.es.json` files idlc itself emits (they target `typescript@^4.5.5` + the auto-generated yarn-workspace `@TestScope/*` scope that's irrelevant for our sample-app harness) and overlays our own template-rendered versions instead. The template `package.json` pins `moment ^2.29.4`, `websocket ^1.0.34`, `@types/node ^22.7.6`, `@types/websocket ^1.0.10`, `tsx ^4.20.0`, `typescript ^5.9.3` — moment+websocket are pulled in via `irt/formatter.ts` / `irt/transport/server/transport.server.websocket.ts` even though the user-domain code under `idltest/` doesn't reach them (Node CJS resolves transitively at module-load time so missing deps fail the run). `tsx` lives in `devDependencies` so `npm install` always provisions a local `./node_modules/.bin/tsx` — we never depend on a globally-installed `tsx`. **Runner probe**: `bun` first, then `npm + node`; the adapter does NOT fall back to a globally-installed `tsx` or to `node --experimental-strip-types` because the latter triggers Node ESM resolution which requires explicit `.ts` extensions on imports (the generated `import {…} from './idltest/dtofields/IntPair'` lines have no extension and Node refuses to fix-up). `tsx` (the resolver-patched form) handles extensionless imports cleanly, and we declare it as a local devDependency so `npm install + ./node_modules/.bin/tsx sample_app.ts` is reproducible. **Acceptance test (passed end-to-end on `wip/necromancy`)**: `./regression-harness/idl-regress --project regression-harness/selftest-corpus/dtofields-only --old git:ea697f5 --new self --lang typescript` → exit 0 with zero divergences across 3 keys. **Wire output verified byte-identical between IMPL-9 (ea697f5) and HEAD on TypeScript backend**: `idltest.dtofields.IntPair.Struct/zero {"x":0,"y":0}`, `IntPair.Struct/mixed {"x":17,"y":-3}`, `WHPair.Struct/default {"h":480,"w":640}` (exactly the same canonical NDJSON as the Scala adapter produces — both backends serialize integer struct fields identically). **Sample-app file-extension carve-out**: `SampleAppGen` previously hardcoded `sample_app.$lang` for both the cache file and meta sidecar, which becomes `sample_app.typescript` for TS — meaningless. Added a small `srcExt` map (`scala → scala`, `typescript → ts`) so the on-disk file is `sample_app.ts` (correct extension for IDE / editor language detection / tsc) while the meta sidecar remains `sample_app.ts.meta`. Path forward when C# arrives in M5: extend the map with `csharp → cs`. **Harness wiring**: `SupportedLangs` set extended to include `"typescript"`; adapter dispatch in `Harness.run` and idlc role mapping in `Harness.runIdlc` each grow a `typescript` case. The `LangAdapter` trait is unchanged — `IdlcResolution.{launcher, runtimeRepoPath, runtimeRepoUri, runtimeVersion}` is language-agnostic; the TS adapter reads only `runtimeVersion` (for interpolation into `package.json`) and ignores `runtimeRepoUri` / `runtimeRepoPath` (no per-sha m2 cache needed — `withRuntime=true` produces a self-contained TS tree). The M2 per-sha resolver still runs Scala-flavored sbt publishM2 invocations for `git:` refs because the resolver is shared between adapters, but the TS adapter never reads the resulting m2/ dir. Future optimization (M5+): skip the runtime publish step when only the TS adapter is requested. **Per-side npm install timing**: ~12 s per side on first run (cold npm cache); ~3 s on second run (warm cache); both nodes_modules/ trees are scratch and removed with the `<runId>/` scratch dir. **selftest.sh extended** with `sanity-ts` (HEAD-vs-HEAD typescript) + `impl9-vs-head-ts` (git:ea697f5 vs HEAD typescript) modes; `selftest-expectations/impl9-vs-head.typescript.json` committed as the baseline (initially empty per plan §9.2 — non-empty diff is a real audit signal). **Bun vs tsx availability on this host**: `bun` not on PATH (not in nix-shell flake). `tsx` not on PATH directly. `npm + node` from `nix-shell` (`nodejs_24`) — adapter selects the `npm` runner and installs `tsx` as a devDependency. **Investigation findings**: (1) **`idlc` emits its own `package.json` + `tsconfig.json`** that we must not preserve. They reference yarn `workspaces` with `packages/@TestScope/*` (an artefact of the auto-publish manifest in `IDLCArgs`'s yarn target naming defaults) and pin `typescript@^4.5.5` which dates from 2022; using our own template avoids both. The skip-filter in `copyTreeFiltered` is keyed on names at the top level only (not nested). (2) **Node ESM resolver vs missing-extension imports**: `node --experimental-strip-types sample_app.ts` fails with `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` on `import { IntPairStruct } from './idltest/dtofields/IntPair'` — generated code never writes `.ts` extensions. `tsx` patches `Module._resolveFilename` so extensionless imports work; `bun run` likewise. We MUST use one of those (decision-tree: `bun` first as it's fastest + zero deps, then `tsx` via local install). (3) **`Path + String` extension on Scala 3.8**: `rawOut.resolveSibling(rawOut.getFileName + ".install")` doesn't compile under 3.8 because `Path` has no `+` operator method — only an extension method `+` exists for some other types and the compiler rightly refuses to synthesize. Fix: explicit `.toString` on `getFileName`. (4) **Per-sha runtime cache is NOT shared for TS**: Documented above. We could short-circuit the M2 publishM2 for git refs when the lang is exclusively TS — deferred to M5. (5) **`bun install` adds a `bun.lockb` binary lockfile** to `workDir`. Harmless under the scratch-dir lifecycle (gone after the run); future cleanup could pass `--frozen-lockfile` once we commit a canonical lockfile. **LOC totals (M4 additions)**: 7 NEW files, 340 LOC: `adapters/TypescriptAdapter.scala` (186) / `templates/typescript/package.json` (19) / `templates/typescript/tsconfig.json` (27) / `prompts/sample-app-typescript.md` (81) / `selftest-corpus/dtofields-only/.idl-regression/sample_app.ts` (19) / `selftest-corpus/dtofields-only/.idl-regression/sample_app.ts.meta` (1) / `selftest-expectations/impl9-vs-head.typescript.json` (7). Edits: `Harness.scala` (+5 lines: typescript SupportedLangs entry, adapter dispatch, idlc role mapping), `SampleAppGen.scala` (+9 lines: `srcExt` map), `selftest.sh` (+24 lines: sanity-ts + impl9-vs-head-ts modes), `README.md` (+10 lines: TS adapter row + file table updates). Well under the plan's 1500 LOC guidance. **No changes to FROZEN harness, build.sbt, or sbt modules**. Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) `<noreply@anthropic.com>`. **Path to M5 (C# adapter)**: (a) `CsharpAdapter` mirroring this structure with `dotnet script` or `dotnet run --project` against an in-place csproj template; (b) `templates/csharp/{Driver.csproj, sample_app.cs}` (the latter just for compile-bootstrap, replaced at run-time by the LLM/hand-rolled sample); (c) `prompts/sample-app-csharp.md`; (d) handle the `idlc :csharp -d enableNUnit=true` flag — the test-harness pattern uses it for NUnit-based assertion fixtures but the regression-harness sample-app shape (stdout TSV emission) doesn't need NUnit, so we'll likely set `enableNUnit=false`; (e) extend the `srcExt` map with `csharp → cs`; (f) add `sanity-cs` + `impl9-vs-head-cs` selftest modes + `impl9-vs-head.csharp.json` expectation baseline.

- **regression-harness M2: git-ref resolution + per-sha launcher/ivy cache** (2026-05-13) — Second milestone of the wire-format regression harness; closes plan §11 M2. Extends `IdlcResolver` (47 → 312 LOC) with `git:<sha|tag|branch>` resolution: parses the ref via `git rev-parse --verify <ref>^{commit}`, materialises a detached worktree under `target/regression-harness/cache/idlc/<short-sha-12>/worktree/` via `git worktree add --detach`, runs `sbt -batch -Dsbt.global.base=<perSha>/.sbt -Dsbt.ivy.home=<perSha>/.ivy2 -Dcoursier.cache=<perSha>/.coursier -Dmaven.repo.local=<perSha>/m2 idealingua-v1-compiler/stage ++ 2.13.18 idealingua-v1-modelJVM/publishM2 idealingua-v1-runtime-rpc-scalaJVM/publishM2`, copies the full sbt-native-packager `target/universal/stage/` tree (bin/ + lib/) to `<perSha>/stage/` (must copy the WHOLE tree — the launcher bash script resolves its classpath via absolute paths relative to its own `bin/`), writes `<perSha>/runtimeVersion.txt`, then removes the worktree (`git worktree remove --force` + `git worktree prune`) unless `--keep-worktrees`. Subsequent invocations hit the cache (`stage/bin/idealingua-v1-compiler` executable + `m2/io/7mind/izumi/<module>_2.13/<version>/` populated) and return immediately. **New file** `IdlcResolution.scala` (24 LOC) — case class carrying `(launcher, runtimeRepoPath, runtimeRepoUri, runtimeVersion)`. The runtime repo URI is `"ivy2Local"` for the self side (publishLocal into `~/.ivy2/local`) and `file:///<perSha>/m2/` for git sides; both are passed through to the scala-cli `using repository` directive via the new `{{RUNTIME_REPOSITORY}}` template substitution. **`LangAdapter` signature change**: `buildAndRun` now takes an `IdlcResolution`, so different sides can render different runtime versions/repos in their per-side scala-cli project. **selftest.sh extended** with `impl9-vs-head` mode running `--old git:ea697f5 --new self --lang scala`; `selftest-expectations/impl9-vs-head.scala.json` committed as the baseline divergence set (initially empty per plan §9.2 — non-empty diff is a real audit signal). **Per-sha cache layout (verified post-acceptance)**: `stage/` 58 MB (full sbt-native-packager tree), `m2/` 5.9 MB (the two `_2.13` runtime artifacts × 4 files each = pom/jar/sources/javadoc), `runtimeVersion.txt` 4.5 KB, `.ivy2/` + `.coursier/` + `.sbt/` retained as empty shells (sbt clears them on completion but the dirs survive — harmless). **Acceptance test (passed end-to-end on `wip/necromancy`)**: `./regression-harness/idl-regress --project regression-harness/selftest-corpus/dtofields-only --old git:ea697f5 --new self --lang scala` → exit 0 with zero divergences across 3 keys. **Wire output verified byte-identical between IMPL-9 (ea697f5) and HEAD on Scala backend**: `idltest.dtofields.IntPair.Struct/zero {"x":0,"y":0}`, `IntPair.Struct/mixed {"x":17,"y":-3}`, `WHPair.Struct/default {"h":480,"w":640}`. Second invocation hits the cache path and skips the build entirely. **Investigation findings + path adjustments**: (1) **Cross-projects + sbt key resolution**: `idealingua-v1-model` and `idealingua-v1-runtime-rpc-scala` are crossProjects (JVMPlatform + JSPlatform); the bare names resolve to aggregator projects that sbt refuses as publish targets ("Not a valid key: idealingua-v1-model"). The JVM concretisation is named `<module>JVM`; `publishTargets(action)` helper appends `JVM/` to each module. (2) **Scala cross-version publishing**: the worktree at `ea697f5` declares `crossScalaVersions := Seq("3.8.3", "2.13.18")` with `scalaVersion := head`; the default `sbt publishM2` only publishes the head (3.8.3 → `<module>_3` artifacts). The scala-cli template targets Scala 2.13.18 (per M1 finding #4, the generated code's `circe-derivation` lock); resolver must invoke `++ 2.13.18` before the publishM2 tasks. (3) **Launcher portability**: the staged bash launcher resolves `lib/*.jar` paths via `dirname $0 → ..` (sbt-native-packager convention); copying ONLY `bin/idealingua-v1-compiler` and removing the worktree breaks `java.lang.ClassNotFoundException: izumi.idealingua.compiler.CommandlineIDLCompiler` at runtime. Fix: copy the whole `target/universal/stage/` tree (added `copyTree` helper using `Files.walk` + `COPY_ATTRIBUTES`). (4) **Stale worktree cleanup**: first-attempt failures leave a registered worktree in `git worktree list` even when the filesystem dir is gone. `addWorktree` now runs `git worktree remove --force` + `git worktree prune` defensively before each `git worktree add` (both tolerant of "not found"). (5) **`Files.isDirectory(c.resolve(".git"))` is false inside an existing worktree** because `.git` is a file (gitdir pointer), not a directory; `detectRepoRoot` correctly walks up to the main repo (`/home/pavel/work/safe/idealingua-v1/`), so the cache lands in the main repo's `target/` regardless of where the agent worktree is. (6) **REAL AUDIT FINDING — IMPL-9 idlc cannot compile HEAD's full `main-tests/source` corpus**: PR-02 F5-fix added `Primitive.TBLOB → Array[Byte]` mapping in `ScalaTypeConverter.toScala` AND the corpus fixture `idltest/blobtest.domain`. At `ea697f5` the F5-fix line 67 case is absent, so when IMPL-9's Scala backend hits TBLOB it falls through to the public `toScala(JavaType)` arm which throws `IDLException: No type->generic mappings implemented yet` (the parameterised `Array[_]` from `toPrimitive(TBLOB)` is not separately handled). This is the wire-format-adjacent contract M2 was designed to surface, and it does. **Mitigation for the M2 selftest harness**: committed an in-tree minimal corpus `regression-harness/selftest-corpus/dtofields-only/` containing only `idltest.dtofields.{IntPair, WHPair}` mixins (a subset both IMPL-9 and HEAD accept), plus the same hand-rolled `sample_app.scala` from M1 (no IDL-shape changes). `selftest.sh impl9-vs-head` points at this corpus; the `sanity` mode keeps the main-tests target (HEAD-vs-HEAD, no compatibility gap). (7) **`+publishM2` cross-build behaviour**: `++ 2.13.18` switches scalaVersion globally for the rest of the sbt session — subsequent `<module>JVM/publishM2` invocations target 2.13. The probe `hasRuntimeArtifacts(repo, version, ivy2)` was tightened to specifically check `<artifact>_2.13/<version>/` (not the generic `<artifact>_<scalaBin>` glob) so a leftover `_3` directory does not produce a false cache-hit. (8) **`scala.util.boundary` discipline**: removed non-local `return` statements from `resolveGit` / `deleteRecursively` per Scala 3.8 warning; `resolveGit` split into a cache-hit fast-path + `buildGit` helper. **LOC totals**: 12 files tracked, 1660 total LOC (M1's 1265 + 395 net), comprising `Harness.scala` (280) / `IdlcResolution.scala` (24, NEW) / `IdlcResolver.scala` (312, +265) / `SampleAppGen.scala` (141) / `LangAdapter.scala` (32, +6) / `adapters/ScalaAdapter.scala` (125, -6 obsolete `readRuntimeVersion`) / `Canonicalize.scala` (233) / `Diff.scala` (153) / `templates/scala/project.scala.template` (12, +1 `{{RUNTIME_REPOSITORY}}`) / `prompts/sample-app-scala.md` (74) / `idl-regress` (6) / `selftest.sh` (57, +36 git-ref mode) / `README.md` (144, +33) / `selftest-expectations/impl9-vs-head.scala.json` (7, NEW) / `selftest-corpus/dtofields-only/source/idltest/dtofields/dtofields.domain` (11, NEW) / `selftest-corpus/dtofields-only/.idl-regression/sample_app.scala` (29 copied from M1) + `.meta` (1). Slightly over the plan's "<1500 LOC" guidance — the IdlcResolver growth covers worktree-add safety, full-stage-copy, cache probing, and version handling; the in-tree corpus + expectations dir add ~50 LOC of supporting test surface. **No changes to FROZEN harness, build.sbt, or sbt modules**. Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) `<noreply@anthropic.com>`. **Path to M3**: (a) `path:<launcher>` ref form in `IdlcResolver` (pre-built launcher; requires sidecar `version.txt` or `--runtime-version` flag); (b) automatic LLM invocation behind `--llm-cli claude|codex|…`; (c) optional `--scala-version` flag to make the `++` cross-build target configurable rather than hardcoded to 2.13.18 (currently coupled to scala-cli template's circe-derivation lock); (d) tighter cache cleanup — strip the empty `.ivy2/.coursier/.sbt` shells after build; (e) full main-tests-corpus sample app once IMPL-9 is past EOL or the corpus is split per-version. **Path to M4**: TypescriptAdapter mirroring `ScalaAdapter` structure with `bun` build/run, `templates/typescript/package.json` + `tsconfig.json`, `prompts/sample-app-typescript.md`. The `IdlcResolution.runtimeRepoUri` carrier is language-agnostic — TS adapter consumes the same triplet but renders a `package.json` `dependencies` entry referencing the per-sha generated TS package (`with-runtime` mode produces a self-contained TS tree, so the M2 m2-repo machinery is not needed for TS; only the launcher resolution is reused).

- **regression-harness M1: scaffold + Scala adapter (manual LLM workflow)** (2026-05-13) — First milestone of the wire-format regression harness designed in `docs/drafts/20260512-regression-harness-plan.md`. Stand-alone tree under `regression-harness/` (NOT an sbt subproject — by D1, to avoid reentrancy when the harness builds the compiler from the same repo). 12 files, 1233 total LOC across `Harness.scala` (279) / `IdlcResolver.scala` (47) / `SampleAppGen.scala` (141) / `LangAdapter.scala` (26) / `adapters/ScalaAdapter.scala` (131) / `Canonicalize.scala` (233 — minimal in-tree JSON parser + canonical re-emitter, no third-party dep on the harness side) / `Diff.scala` (153) / `templates/scala/project.scala.template` (11) / `prompts/sample-app-scala.md` (74) / `idl-regress` (6 bash wrapper) / `selftest.sh` (21) / `README.md` (111). **Scope**: `--old self --new self --lang scala` only (HEAD-vs-HEAD sanity). `git:<sha>` / `path:<launcher>` / typescript / csharp deferred to M2 (NotImplementedError stubs surface a clear "M2 scope" message). **User simplification (locked at orchestrator's request)**: NO automatic LLM invocation. On first run, harness emits a self-contained prompt to `target/regression-harness/<runId>/sample-app-prompt.scala.md` + sentinel `PROMPT_READY.txt` and exits **code 3** with operator instructions. Operator runs the LLM externally and drops `sample_app.scala` + meta sidecar at `<project>/.idl-regression/`. Second invocation reuses the cached sample. **Acceptance test (passed end-to-end on `wip/necromancy`)**: (a) first invocation with no cache → exit 3, prompt and sentinel materialised; (b) hand-rolled 3-fixture sample (`IntPair.Struct` × 2 scenarios + `WHPair.Struct` × 1) dropped at the cache path → second invocation builds + runs both sides under scala-cli, canonicalizes NDJSON, diffs → exit 0 with zero divergences across 3 keys. Wire output verified canonical: `{"x":0,"y":0}` / `{"x":17,"y":-3}` / `{"h":480,"w":640}` (keys lexicographically sorted by Canonicalize, no whitespace). **Investigation findings**: (1) **`sbt-native-packager` launcher script eats `-d`/`-v` short flags** as its own debug/verbose toggles before forwarding to the JVM application; the residual line `[residual] arg = 'layout=PLAIN'` is misleading — `-d` is consumed by the bash launcher, so the application sees `:scala layout=PLAIN` (no preceding `-d`) and falls back to the default SBT layout, which then dies with `SBT layout requires at least one Scala version`. Fix: long-form options `--define=layout=PLAIN` and `--disable-zip` instead of `-d layout=PLAIN` and `-nz`. Documented inline in `Harness.runIdlc`. (2) **scala-cli `using resolver` directive renamed to `using repository`** in scala-cli ≥1.0; template updated. (3) **scala-cli picks up every `*.scala` file in the input tree as a source** — including templates. Renamed `templates/scala/project.scala` → `project.scala.template` so scala-cli skips it; `ScalaAdapter` materialises it back to `project.scala` after substitution at runtime. (4) **Sample app must target Scala 2.13.18 for M1**: generated Scala uses `import _root_.io.circe.derivation.{deriveDecoder, deriveEncoder}` which is the Scala-2-only path (per `project/Versions.scala`, `circe_derivation = "0.13.0-M5"` only has 2.13 artifacts in scope; Scala 3 uses `circe-generic`). Switching the scala-cli template to Scala 2.13.18 + adding `circe-derivation` and `circe-generic-extras` resolves cleanly. M2 will need to either (a) emit a Scala-3-compatible variant of the generated code or (b) keep the harness on Scala 2.13.18 indefinitely. (5) **Runtime + model both need `publishLocal`**: `idealingua-v1-runtime-rpc-scala` does NOT depend on `idealingua-v1-model`, yet the generated code references `izumi.idealingua.runtime.model.IDLGeneratedType` which lives in the model module. The scala-cli template depends on both via `ivy2Local`. Documented as a pre-flight step in README. (6) **Compiler launcher path**: `idealingua-v1/idealingua-v1-compiler/target/universal/stage/bin/idealingua-v1-compiler` (matches the path the plan §2 D2 predicted). (7) **scala-cli availability**: present on this host at `/nix/store/dgn3ch0v61rfb23lyxxq7sagp89nq6hv-scala-cli-1.10.1/bin/scala-cli` via the project's nix-shell. Older than current upstream (1.13.0) but supports every directive the template uses. Documented in README under requirements. (8) **Non-local returns are now deprecated in Scala 3.8** — converted `Harness.run` and `Canonicalize.canonicalize` to use `scala.util.boundary`/`break` to silence warnings cleanly. **Exit-code surface**: 0 (no divergences) / 1 (divergences, with `--fail-on-divergence` default) / 2 (build or setup failure) / 3 (sample app not cached — prompt emitted, operator must run LLM) / 126 (CLI usage error). **Cache key**: `idl_sha256` over the IDL tree (recursive `.domain`/`.model` walk, sorted, sha256), stored in `<project>/.idl-regression/sample_app.<lang>.meta`; stale cache surfaces a "use --regen-sample-app" message. **Per-run scratch** lives under `target/regression-harness/<runId>/` which is already gitignored via the top-level `target/` rule. **No new sbt tasks**; the harness is fully out-of-tree. The FROZEN `idealingua-v1-test-harness/` module is untouched (M1 mirrors conventions, doesn't import). **Path to M2**: (a) `IdlcResolver.{resolveGit, resolvePath}` — `git worktree add` + `sbt stage` cached by sha; (b) `TypescriptAdapter` (bun-based template) + `CsharpAdapter` (dotnet template); (c) optional `--llm-cli` for automatic invocation with the manual-prompt path as the default; (d) replace the hard-coded `CirceVersion` constant in `ScalaAdapter` with a manifest emitted by sbt; (e) auto-generate full main-tests-corpus sample app (the M1 hand-rolled 3-fixture stub is a placeholder, not coverage).

- **CI: remove `:test-pb` scaffolding + `negative/` filter in shellspec defs walk** (2026-05-13) — Closes the `test-compiler` matrix regression. The `:test-pb` row in `.github/workflows/build.yml` invoked `mdl ... :test-pb`, which ran `sbt "... idealingua-v1-compiler/run ... :protobuf"`; the `:protobuf` CLI role was hard-removed in **IMPL-8** (commit `4953dac`) — `IDLLanguage.parse("protobuf")` now throws `MatchError` and no `LP.languageOpts` schema accepts it. The job has been unreachable on CI since IMPL-8. **Part A — `:test-pb` retirement (4 files)**: (1) `.github/workflows/build.yml:65` — matrix shrinks to `[ ':test-scala', ':test-cs', ':test-ts' ]`. (2) `.mdl/defs/tests.md` — entire `# action: test-pb` section deleted; aggregator `# action: test` drops `dep action.test-pb`. (3) `.mdl/spec/pb_spec.sh` — file deleted (Protobuf shellspec describe). (4) `.mdl/lib/builders.sh` — `test_pb_prj()` helper deleted (the function still referenced the dead `:protobuf` role + `protoc --java_out`). **Part B — `negative/` filter regression fix (3 files)**: `:test-cs` / `:test-ts` / `:test-scala` shellspec drivers iterated `defs/*` via `find -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d` and dispatched each to `test_*_prj "$1"`, which expects `$1/source/` to exist for `--source=$1/source`. The `defs/negative/` tree added in **PR-03.5** (commit `e3405ed`) layers as `negative/<category>/<case>/<files>` with no top-level `source/` subdir — so every `:test-{scala,cs,ts}` run since PR-03.5 would have failed on the `negative` row regardless of the protobuf removal. The three shellspec drivers (`scala_spec.sh`, `cs_spec.sh`, `ts_spec.sh`) now use `find -maxdepth 2 -mindepth 2 -type d -name source -printf '%h\n' | sort -u`, selecting only def dirs that satisfy the `--source=$1/source` contract: `main-tests`, `overlays`, `subdir`, `overlay-version` (the four pre-PR-03.5 dirs; matches develop's CI surface). `negative/` is consumed only by `NegativeSpec` in the test harness module, which does its own `loadCorpus` walk against `.must-reject` markers. **CLI smoke verification (post-IMPL-13 state)**: `sbt '++ 2.13.18; idealingua-v1-compiler/run --root=... --source=.../source --overlay=.../overlay --target=/tmp/idlc-smoke :scala -d layout=SBT -d sbt.scalaVersions=2.13.18'` → success; same for `:typescript -d layout=YARN` → success; same for `:csharp -d layout=NUGET -d enableNUnit=true` → success. All three CLI surfaces accept the flags the shellspec builders pass. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok (byte-equal, no regeneration), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. **CI matrix soundness**: the post-change `test-compiler` matrix is `{ :test-scala, :test-cs, :test-ts } × { 2.13 }`. With the `negative/` filter in place every shellspec iteration runs against a def dir that satisfies the source-overlay layout contract. The `--scala-version=2.13`-only matrix is unchanged (CI never ran the integration suite on Scala 3; the FROZEN harness contracts run cross-build via the `build` job). **No protobuf code touched** (deleted in IMPL-8). **No new tests** introduced; one CLI role row removed; three shellspec parameter expressions tightened to the source-bearing contract.

- **PR-02 F-TextTree M8f: retire scala.meta from toscala/domain/ (carrier flips String-everywhere)** (2026-05-12) — Final milestone of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite; closes F-Scala-TextTree. **Audit-clean state**: `grep -rn "scala\.meta\|scalameta" toscala/domain/` returns EMPTY. **Scope**: every renderer in `toscala/domain/` now composes `TextTree[ScalaRefHandle]`, calls `.mapRender(resolver.resolve)` at the product boundary, and hands rendered Scala-source strings to `CogenProduct.fromTexts` / `EnumProduct.fromTexts` / `AdtProduct.fromTexts` / `AdtElementProduct.fromTexts` / `CogenServiceProduct.fromTexts` / `CogenServiceProduct.Pair.fromTexts`. The carrier (in `toscala/products/`) owns the String → Defn parse boundary internally. **Files deleted**: `domain/DomainScalaParseBack.scala` (88 LOC). **Files added**: `tools/ScalaTextHelpers.scala` (~80 LOC — keyword-escape `escapeIdent` / `escapeTypeIdent`, tree-to-source `renderTree`, parse-back helpers `parseStat` / `parseTrait` / `parseObject` / `parseClass` / `parseDef` / `parseDefn` / `parseInit`; all `scala.meta` reach concentrated here, outside the audit boundary). **Renderers flipped to text**: `mkTrait` now returns `String` (with AnyVal mixin pre-spliced into the trait header text); `withAnyForStruct` / `withAnyForInterface` return `List[String]`; `DomainServiceMethodProduct.{inputDefnText, outputDefnText, defStructsText, withCirceText, withAdtCirceText, renderShim, renderOutput}` flip from `List[Defn]` to `List[String]`. `EnumProduct.elements` flipped from `List[(Term.Name, Defn)]` to `List[(String, Defn)]`. **Carrier additions**: 6 new `fromTexts` factory methods in `products/CogenProduct.scala` parse the String inputs back to `Defn` once at carrier construction time; the `CogenProductSplice` slot machinery is unchanged. **scala.meta usage relocated**: now lives in `toscala/products/CogenProduct.scala` (carrier slot-splice + parse-back via `ScalaTextHelpers`), `toscala/products/RenderableCogenProduct.scala` (slot machinery), `toscala/tools/ModuleTools.scala` (final `dialect(tree).syntax` per-module emit), `toscala/tools/ScalaMetaTools.scala` (the `Defn.prependBase` / `appendDefinitions` extension methods consumed by `CogenProductSplice`), `toscala/tools/ScalaTextHelpers.scala` (the centralised helper). The deepest call into scalameta from `toscala/domain/` is now a `ScalaTextHelpers.escapeIdent("name")` invocation that consumes a String and returns a String. **Pitfalls surfaced + resolved**: (1) **Position-preservation on parse-back-then-print**: scalameta's printer preserves source layout when the parsed AST node retains positional info from the input string (no AST-node construction strips positions). Previously `mkTrait` returned a `Defn.Trait` produced by `prependBase` — that `.copy()` stripped positions, so the printer re-rendered canonically (multi-line body). New code splices the AnyVal base into the header text, parses the string, and the position-preserving printer prints the body single-line, breaking 47 goldens on round 1. Fix: emit the canonical Scala 3 trait body layout up front — `{ def f: T }` on one line for single-stat bodies, `{\n  def f: T\n  def g: T\n}` multi-line for multi-stat. (2) **`CogenServiceProduct` historical field-swap bug**: the legacy positional ctor `CogenServiceProduct(server, client, ServerPair, ClientPair, methods, codecs, imports)` was called with positional args, but the case-class field order is `(…, clientWrapped, serverWrapped, …)` — the bug-for-bug behaviour is that `clientWrapped` actually holds the server-wrapped pair, and vice-versa, baked into 6 service goldens. M8f's named-arg construction in `fromTexts` initially "fixed" the swap and inverted Client/Server class emit order. Restored bug-for-bug behaviour by explicitly cross-wiring `clientWrapped = serverWrapped` / `serverWrapped = clientWrapped` inside `CogenServiceProduct.fromTexts`. (3) **AdtElementProduct type alias**: `AdtRenderer` needs to name `AdtElementProduct[Defn.Class]` without referencing `Defn.Class` text. Added a `CogenProduct.AdtElementClassProduct` type alias. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` byte-equal across the corpus, `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. **F-TextTree fully closed**: TS (M1..M2) + C# (M3) + Scala (M4..M8f) all migrated.

- **PR-02 F-TextTree M8e: port composite scaffolding off scala.meta** (2026-05-12) — Eighth milestone, sub-cycle "e" of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite; **scaffolder retirement** (after M8a/b/c/d landed the extensions and Circe). **Scope**: three scaffolder files + the consumers that splice their output. Per-file LOC before → after: `domain/DomainScalaStruct.scala` 233 → 235 (+2); `domain/DomainCompositeStructure.scala` 193 → 252 (+59); `domain/DomainServiceContext.scala` 82 → 94 (+12); plus `types/ScalaField.scala` 24 → 33 (+9), `types/ScalaTypeConverter.scala` 188 → 192 (+4), and consumer-side `renderS30`-call removals at `DomainCompositeRenderer.scala` (+/-3), `DomainInterfaceRenderer.scala` (+/-9), `DomainIdRenderer.scala` (+/-12), `DomainServiceMethodProduct.scala` (+/-10), `DomainServiceRenderer.scala` (+/-8). Total: 10 files, 221 insertions / 121 deletions (~100 net). **Carriers flipped**: `ScalaField(name: Term.Name, fieldType: Type, ...)` → `ScalaField(name: String, nameSafe: String, fieldType: String, ...)` — `name` is the raw field identifier, `nameSafe` is the Scala-3 keyword-escaped form (via `dialect(Scala30)(Term.Name(_)).syntax`, the same path M8c uses inside cast extensions), `fieldType` is the pre-rendered `typeFull.toString`. `DomainCompositeStructure.{decls, names, constructors}` flip from `List[Term.Param]` / `List[Term.Name]` / `List[Defn.Def]` to `List[String]`; consumers (`DomainCompositeRenderer`, `DomainInterfaceRenderer.{renderInterface, mkTrait}`, `DomainServiceMethodProduct.Input.{signatureText, sigCallText, sigDirectCallText, inputDefn}`, `DomainIdRenderer.renderIdentifier`) drop the `renderS30(_)` boundary call and splice strings verbatim. `DomainServiceContext.{IO2.n, F.{t,p}, Ctx.{t,p}, methodImport}` flip from `Term.Name` / `Type.Name` / `Type.Param` / `Import` to `String`; `DomainServiceRenderer` constructs a local `Type.Name(c.F.t)` for the residual `parameterize(List[Type])` callsites against `IRTDispatcher` / `svcClientTpe` (those scaffolders are outside M8e scope). **Quasiquotes retired from the three target files**: zero `q"..."` / `t"..."` / `param"..."` / `tparam"..."` / `init"..."` macros remain across `DomainScalaStruct`, `DomainCompositeStructure`, `DomainServiceContext`. **scala.meta residue**: `import scala.meta.Term` in `DomainCompositeStructure` + `DomainIdRenderer` + `DomainScalaStruct` + `ScalaTypeConverter` for `Term.Name(_)`-feeding-`renderS30` keyword escape (matches M8c/M8d pattern); `DomainServiceRenderer` keeps `scala.meta.Type.Name(_)` for the two `parameterize` callsites; `dialect(Scala30)(...).syntax` invocation inside `ScalaTypeConverter` requires materialising the dialect into a `val` because the `XtensionDialectApply` implicit doesn't bind cleanly on a fresh inline reference under Scala 2 (compile error "scala.meta.Dialect does not take parameters" if called as `scala.meta.dialects.Scala30(Term.Name(_)).syntax`). **Pitfalls surfaced + resolved**: (1) **Right-grouped `&&` precedence under multi-operand assert.** The legacy `Params.assertion` fold `assertions.tail.foldLeft(assertions.head)((a, acc) => q"$acc && $a")` produces a *right-grouped* `Term.ApplyInfix` tree (`x2 && (x1 && x0)`), which scalameta's printer parenthesizes as `(x2) && ((x1) && (x0))` because `&&` is left-associative by default-parse. First port used a flat left-to-right `(a) && (b) && (c)` shape, breaking 8 goldens (`diamonds/DTO1.scala`, `dtofields/Point.scala`, `inheritance/NotiWithFileRevision.scala`, four `domain01/*Mixin|*Object` files, `domain02/DTO1.scala`) — every multi-parent-distinct-source mirror constructor. Fix: track fold-iteration count, wrap the accumulator in parens on every iteration past the first so the running `&&` chain surfaces the right-grouping convention. Single-assert and 2-assert paths unchanged (those don't trigger the bug — 2-assert is just `(a) && (b)` flat). (2) **`scala.meta.dialects.Scala30(Term.Name).syntax` inline form fails under Scala 2 compile.** The `XtensionDialectApply` implicit that adds `apply[T <: Tree](tree: T): T` needs the dialect bound to a `val` in scope; called inline as `scala.meta.dialects.Scala30(Term.Name(...))` it dispatches to `Dialect.apply(String): Input.String` instead. Fix: route every keyword-escape call through `DomainScalaParseBack.renderS30(Term.Name(_))` (which already encapsulates the val-bound dialect). `ScalaTypeConverter` lives in the `types` subpackage and can't import `DomainScalaParseBack` (which is `private[toscala]` in `domain`) cleanly, so it materialises its own `private val dialect = scala.meta.dialects.Scala30` and calls `dialect(Term.Name(_)).syntax`. (3) **`methodImport` Scala-3 dialect rendering.** The legacy `Import(List(Importer(pkgTerm, List(Importee.Rename(Name(typeName), Name("_M"))))))` tree printed under Scala 3 as `import _root_.<pkg>.<TypeName> as _M` (Scala 3 `as` renaming, no braces for single-rename importee — verified via golden `idltest/services/TestService.scala:69`). Direct string composition emits this form verbatim. (4) **`tparam"Or[?, ?]"` lowers to `Or[+_, +_]` under Scala 3.** The existential `?` in type-param position becomes `+_` variant wildcards at print. Verified via golden `idltest/services/TestService.scala:11` (`trait TestServiceServer[Or[+_, +_], C]`). Replacement `F.p: String = "Or[+_, +_]"` matches verbatim. (5) **`distinctBy` already on stdlib `List` in 2.13.** The `import izumi.fundamentals.collections.IzCollections._` inside `DomainCompositeStructure.constructors` is now unused (the only IzCollections member touched was `distinctBy`, which 2.13 provides natively); dropped. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` byte-equal across the 28-domain corpus (multi-assert precedence fix surfaced in round 1; round 2 clean), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42 (every existing scaffolder test continues to assert on rendered text — return-type flips from `Defn.Def` to `String` are transparent to the substring-assertion pattern). **Path to M8f (final parse-back retirement + scala.meta cleanup)**: with M8e's call paths in place, the only remaining `DomainScalaParseBack.parseStat` / `parseInit` callers are `products.CogenProductSplice` (slot-parse-back at carrier render time for `defnAnyvalBases` / `companionCirceBases` / `companionCasts` / `siblings`) and `DomainInterfaceRenderer.scala:139` (one residual `parseStat(structCirce.defnText)` in a never-taken fallback arm of `implAugmentedDefns`). The renderer-side `renderS30` calls that survive M8e are: (a) `mirrorInterface.map(renderS30(_))` in `DomainCompositeRenderer` (the mirror trait is still `Defn.Trait` from `mkTrait`); (b) `runtime.Import` rendering in service/composite/interface renderers (these are not scala.meta — they're the project-local `Import` ADT); (c) per-renderer pre-computed `ctx.rt.X.{init, termBase}` strings via `renderS30(init())`. M8f collapses these by: (1) flipping `mkTrait` to return `String`; (2) flipping `CogenProduct` carriers to String-everywhere with a final `parseStat` at `ModuleTools.toSource` boundary, or — preferred for M1.5 alignment — adopting `TextTree[ScalaRefHandle]` end-to-end and replacing `.syntax` with `.mapRender(resolver.resolve)`. Either path retires `DomainScalaParseBack` entirely.
- **PR-02 F-TextTree M8d: port DomainCirceTranslatorExtensionBase off scala.meta** (2026-05-12) — Eighth milestone, sub-cycle "d" of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite; **WIRE-FORMAT-CRITICAL** per-extension `scala.meta` retirement (after M8a/M8b/M8c). **Scope**: single-file diff on `domain/extensions/DomainCirceTranslatorExtensionBase.scala` (521 → 599 LOC, +78). Replaces every `q"trait XCirce { … }"` quasiquote body — covering `withParseable` (Identifier/Enum), `withDerivedStructCore` (DTO + impl-struct, deriveEncoder/deriveDecoder + Scala-3 AnyVal `forProduct1` branch), `emitForMethodStruct` (service-method-output unwrap arm), `emitForAdt` (tagged-union Encoder.AsObject.instance + Decoder.instance for-comprehension), `emitForInterface` (implementor `Map(wireId -> v)` discriminator) — with direct Scala source string composition. **Quasiquotes retired**: every `q"..."` macro across the file is gone. **scala.meta residue**: `import scala.meta.{Term, Type}` for `DomainScalaParseBack.renderS30(Term.Name(_))` (Scala-3 keyword-safe field-name rendering) + `renderS30(Type.Name(_))` (trait-name slot, identical safety); `mkCirceTrait` retains `ctx.conv.toScala(ownerId).sibling(name).init()` followed by `renderS30(init)` for the `companionCirceBases` slot init string (legacy `Owner.StructCirce` qualification — bare `StructCirce` broke parity at `idltest/algebraics/AFace.scala:35`). **Wire-format parity preserved**: `runWireFixtures` (26 Scala + 20 TS + 21 C#) and `runCrossLangInterop` (118/10/6) both green on Scala 2.13.18 AND 3.8.3 — the gates for JSON wire-byte equivalence. **Pitfalls surfaced + resolved**: (1) **Trailing-underscore val-name parses as `_:` typed-wildcard pattern.** Legacy `q"implicit val ${Pat.Var(Term.Name("encodeName_stored_"))}: …"` rendered as `"implicit val encodeName_stored_ : T = …"` — note the **space before the colon**, scalameta's disambiguation for trailing-`_` identifiers (verified via in-tree probe test). Direct string composition `s"implicit val $name: $tpe"` produces `encodeName_stored_:` which the parser treats as `encodeName_stored` + `_:` (typed-wildcard pattern) and rejects with "illegal start of simple pattern". Fix: `valName(s)` helper renders the name via `renderS30(Term.Name(s))` and appends a trailing space when the rendered form ends in `_`. Surfaced by `idltest.identifiers.Name_stored_` in domain02. (2) **Scalameta preserves source tokens on parse.** Once a string is `parseStat`'d, scalameta keeps the original tokens (e.g. `import a._` stays `_`, multi-statement blocks stay compact, etc.) and the dialect-printer only re-renders the structural envelope. The byte-equality contract is therefore between **the source string we emit** and **the final printed byte sequence after parse+reprint**, not between our string and the AST canonical form. Confirmed by another in-tree probe: `q"import _root_.io.circe.syntax._".syntax` under Scala 3 dialect prints `import _root_.io.circe.syntax.*` (synthetic AST); the same string parsed back preserves `_`. Resolution: emit `*` in source strings (Scala 3 dialect accepts both at parse, prints `*` at reprint). Applied to every wildcard import inside emitted traits. Same logic applied to: (a) case-arm formatting — emit each `case … =>\n      <body>` across two indented lines to match the canonical printer output; (b) for-comprehension form — emit `for (a <- b; c = d; e <- f) yield …` (paren form, semicolon-separated generators) rather than `for { … }`; (c) for-comprehension yield body — Adt uses `yield { result }` (block), Interface uses `yield result` (bare); (d) `Decoder.instance(c => { … })` outer body — `val maybeContent = … .toRight(…)` stays on a single line; (e) no blank line between `import` and `implicit val` (the legacy q-printer omits blank lines inside trait bodies); (f) `KeyDecoder[T]` body inlined onto a single line. (3) **Init-text qualification.** First port simplified `CirceTrait.initText` to the bare trait name (`StructCirce`), reasoning that the `Init` would re-qualify via the enclosing companion at parse-back time. **Wrong**: legacy emitted `Owner.StructCirce` (the `ctx.conv.toScala(ownerId).sibling(name).init()` path resolves to `Type.Select(Type.Singleton(termRef), Type.Name(name))` for impl-DTO mirrors, whose `renderS30` output is the qualified path). Restored the `sibling().init()` → `renderS30` round-trip for `initText`. Surfaced by `AFace.scala:35` golden mismatch. (4) **Unwrap codec uses `termName` not `termFull`.** Service-method-output ephemeral codec body legacy emitted `v.as[T].map(d => HelloOutput(d))` (bare `termName`), not `TestService.HelloOutput(d)` (`termFull`). First port used `termFull`, breaking 8 service-output goldens (`TestService.scala`, `TestBuzzer.scala`); fix routes through `stype.termName.toString`. (5) **`Lit.String` escape handling in `DecodingFailure` text.** `s"…"`-interpolation in Scala source preserves `\"` as escape; my source strings embed `\\"` (double-backslash) which Scala's own `s""` interpolation processes once to `\"`, which then appears literally in the parsed AST as the escape — matching the legacy q-printer's output. No `verbatim(_)` analog needed because the standard `s""` interpolation already preserves the escape semantics correctly. **`mkCirceTrait` signature change**: `defn: Defn.Trait` → `defnText: String` — the parameter now carries pre-rendered source text. `ownerId` retained for the sibling-init computation. **Carrier untouched**: `CirceTrait(name, defnText, initText)` API unchanged; downstream consumers (`DomainScalaTranslator.{emitEnum, emitIdentifier, emitDto, emitInterface, emitAdt}`, `DomainServiceMethodProduct.{withCirce, withAdtCirce}`, `DomainInterfaceRenderer.implStructExtensionsChain`) consume the same three-String triple. **Helper additions**: `valName(s)` (val-name escape + trailing-`_` space-pad), `typeName(s)` (trait-name slot rendering via `Type.Name`), `quoteString(s)` (manual `"`/`\\` escape replacing the prior `Lit.String(s)` → `renderS30` call). **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` byte-equal across the 28-domain corpus (all Circe goldens — `JLArray.scala`, `JSONLike.scala`, `CouponData.scala`, `AdtTester.scala`, plus every other DTO/ADT/Interface/Enum/Identifier — match across both dialects), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp (**wire-format gate**), `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded (**cross-language gate**), `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42 (6-case `DomainCirceExtensionSpec` continues to assert substring-on-rendered-text). **Path to M8e (composite scaffolding retirement)**: `DomainScalaStruct.scala` (233 LOC) and `DomainCompositeStructure.scala` (193 LOC) still emit `scala.meta.{Term.Param, Defn.Def, Init}` consumed by the now-String-native extensions via `renderS30(_)`. With M8d's call paths in place, M8e can migrate those scaffolders to pure String composition without cascading boundary churn — every extension consumer already accepts String. M8f retires the residual `scala.meta` reach from `DomainScalaParseBack` itself: once M8e lands, the only remaining `parseStat`/`parseInit` callers are `CogenProductSplice` and the `siblings`-list parse-back in `DomainInterfaceRenderer.scala:139`; collapsing those means CogenProduct flips to either (a) String-everywhere with a final `parseStat` at `ModuleTools.toSource` boundary, or (b) `TextTree[ScalaRefHandle]` end-to-end with the renderer's `.syntax` call replaced by `.mapRender`. Option (b) is the M1.5 alignment goal — completing F-TextTree.
- **PR-02 F-TextTree M8c: port 3 cast extensions off scala.meta** (2026-05-12) — Eighth milestone, sub-cycle "c" of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite; second per-extension `scala.meta` retirement (after M8b). **Scope**: 3-file diff on `domain/extensions/DomainCast{Similar,Up,DownExpand}Extension.scala`. Replaces the M8a-era `q"implicit object … extends ${ctx.rt.Cast.parameterize(...).init()} { override def convert(...) = $code }"` quasiquote shape with direct Scala source string composition. Per-extension LOC delta: `DomainCastSimilarExtension.scala` 168 → 173 (+5); `DomainCastUpExtension.scala` 281 → 282 (+1); `DomainCastDownExpandExtension.scala` 130 → 151 (+21, growth concentrated in the `assert((a) && (b))`-precedence-preserving guard + per-source-name escape calls). **Quasiquotes retired**: zero `q"..."` `scala.meta` macros remain across the three files; the only `scala.meta` residue is the `import scala.meta.Term` used to feed `DomainScalaParseBack.renderS30(Term.Name(...))` for backtick-escaping field/parameter names that happen to be Scala 3 reserved words (`package`, `export`, etc. — surfaced by `TestService.SimpleGoReservedInput.package` in `scala/izumi/test/domain02/TestInterface2.scala`). Direct string composition without escape produced `package = _value.package` which is invalid Scala; routing through `renderS30(Term.Name("package"))` yields `` `package` = _value.`package` ``. **Cast base reference**: `ctx.rt.Cast.parameterize(List(thisFull, parentFull)).typeFull.toString` (and `ctx.rt.Extend.parameterize(...).typeFull.toString` in the DownExpand path) replaces the prior `.init().toString` — `typeFull` produces the same `IRTCast[A, B]` text without invoking `Init`'s parens-emitting branch (which only matters for non-empty constructor args; here we pass none). **Pitfalls surfaced**: (1) **Operator-precedence regression on `assert(a && b)`** — legacy `q"$acc && $a"` over `q"$x ne null"` operands produced `Term.ApplyInfix(_, &&, _)` over `Term.ApplyInfix(_, ne, _)`, which scalameta prints with disambiguating parens around each operand: `((x ne null) && (y ne null))`. Plain string concatenation `"x ne null && y ne null"` parses to `x ne (null && y) ne null` because `&&` has lower precedence than `ne` and `ne` is left-associative — the parse-back boundary surfaces this as a non-byte-equal diff at `scala/izumi/test/domain02/TestInterface2.scala` (the only multi-local-field downcast-extend site in the corpus). Fix: wrap each `ne null` operand with explicit parens before `&&`-joining, preserving the legacy printer's parens convention. Single-assertion case keeps no operand parens (matching scalameta's printer: single-arg `assert(x ne null)` has no operand parens). (2) **Reserved-word field name escape** — described above. (3) **Empty-constructor-args**: `targetFull(...)` with empty `ctorArgs` produces `Foo()` which parses identically to legacy `q"$f(..$Nil)"`. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` byte-equal across the 28-domain corpus (`TestInterface2.scala` parens-precedence fix surfaced and resolved in cycle 1; all 215 Scala goldens × 2 dialects match), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42 (6 cast-extension specs continue to assert substring-on-rendered-text, return-type unchanged at `List[String]`). **Path to M8d (DomainCirceTranslatorExtensionBase — 521 LOC, wire-format critical)**: the Circe extension owns `Encoder.AsObject[T]` / `Decoder[T]` derivation including ADT branch discrimination (`Lit.String` branch names), `Term.Match` cases over implementor lists, `Pat.Var` LHS for implicit val names, the per-domain `<Carrier>Codecs` outer trait, and the `forProduct1`-based AnyVal-flat codec path. M8d is the deepest dependency: byte-parity defects there shift JSON wire bytes (not just Scala source). Approach: same direct-string composition as M8c, with extra care for (1) the `Term.Match` arm shapes — each `case <Branch>(_) => ` arm must preserve scalameta's `case Pat.Bind(_)` printing convention; (2) the `Lit.String("...")` literal escapes for `DecodingFailure` text — `verbatim(_)` analog is needed since `s"$expr"` interpolation will re-escape `\"`; (3) the indentation discipline inside derived Encoder/Decoder bodies (Scala 3 indent-sensitive parser quirk M7 surfaced) — every nested splice point needs `.shift(N).trim` if using TextTree composition, or `\n      ` literal indent if plain strings. M8e (retire `DomainScalaParseBack.{parseStat, parseInit}`) lands once M8d completes and the last consumer disappears. M8f retires the remaining `scala.meta` imports.

- **PR-02 F-TextTree M8b: port DomainAnyvalExtension off scala.meta** (2026-05-12) — Eighth milestone, sub-cycle "b" of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite; first per-extension `scala.meta` retirement. **Scope**: single-file diff on `domain/extensions/DomainAnyvalExtension.scala`. Replaces the M8a-era `doModify(ctx, base, modify)` body — `List(DomainScalaParseBack.renderS30(ctx.conv.toScala(JavaType(Seq.empty, base)).init()))` — with direct string composition: `List(base)`. Justification (M8a executor's hand-off, verified at call site): `JavaType(Seq.empty, base)` produces a no-package, no-parameter scaffold whose `ScalaTypeImpl.init()` constructs `Init(Type.Name(base), Name.Anonymous(), Nil)`; `renderS30` of that under the Scala 3 dialect emits the bare base name (`"AnyVal"`). The round-trip is byte-stable but uninformative — every input that takes the `modify = true` arm rendered to a single token. **Public arms flipped**: `withAnyvalForComposite`, `withAnyvalForMethodStruct`, `withAnyvalForIdentifier` — all three return `List[String]` and now compose text directly. **Renderer-internal arms preserved on `Init`**: `withAnyForStruct` (consumed by `DomainInterfaceRenderer.scala:235` via `TraitProduct(parsed).defn.prependBase(...)`) and `withAnyForInterface` (test-only consumer) keep `List[Init]` because `mkTrait`'s `prependBase` API still operates on `scala.meta.Init`. Their helper `doModifyInit(ctx, base, modify)` retains the `ctx.conv.toScala(JavaType(Seq.empty, base)).init()` call — it builds the `Init` directly without parse-back, so the only `scala.meta` reach left in the file is the `Init` return type itself (consumed downstream by `prependBase`). **Imports trimmed**: dropped `DomainScalaParseBack` import (no longer used in this file — other consumers remain), narrowed `import scala.meta.*` to `import scala.meta.Init`. **Signature stability**: `withAnyvalForIdentifier(ctx, id)` keeps the `ctx` parameter for call-site symmetry with the other `withAnyvalFor*` arms (suppressed via `@nowarn("msg=parameter ctx")` — same pattern as the existing `@nowarn` on `canBeAnyValField`); no caller change. **LOC delta**: 171 → 187 (+16; the extra lines are M8b-justifying doc comments on `doModify` / `doModifyInit` / `withAnyvalForIdentifier`, not new code). The actual `scala.meta` retirement removes 1 production call (`DomainScalaParseBack.renderS30(...)` invocation) and the corresponding round-trip dependency. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` byte-equal across the 28-domain corpus (every `AnyVal` / `Any` splice site re-prints identically because the M8a parse-back roundtrip on `"AnyVal"` / `"Any"` was already byte-stable — switching to direct string composition emits the same single token), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42 (the 5-case `DomainAnyvalExtensionSpec` continues to assert `.size` only — return-type-agnostic). **Path to M8c (3 cast extensions: CastSimilar / CastUp / CastDownExpand)**: the cast extensions own the `q"implicit object Foo extends Cast[...] { override def convert(...) = $code }"` shape; lowering to `s"..."` template literals requires (1) inspecting the per-handler shape (~163 / ~274 / ~128 LOC respectively per M8a's tally) to enumerate the splice points (`Cast` base type, struct field list, parent/child type names), (2) auditing `DomainScalaStruct.fromFlat` consumers inside each extension to confirm no further `Tree` interplay (the cast extensions consume `ScalaStruct` field projections, not raw `Defn`), (3) confirming the cast-helper output sites still consume the rendered `String` via the `companionCasts: List[String]` slot M8a established — `CogenProductSplice.parseStat` handles parse-back at carrier render time, so dropping the per-extension `q"..."` quasiquote keeps the carrier untouched. The Scala 3 indentation pitfall M7 surfaced needs re-checking for any cast helpers that splice multi-statement `convert` bodies; the AnyVal arm was indent-trivial (zero-arg `Init`). M8d (Circe — the deepest dependency) and M8e (retire `DomainScalaParseBack`) remain scoped as in M8a's path note.

- **PR-02 F-TextTree M8a: CogenProduct String-API with Defn-printer internal (extensions push strings)** (2026-05-12) — Eighth milestone (sub-cycle "a") of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite; first cycle on the extension layer. **Refactor only — no scala.meta retirement**: extensions still build `q"..."` quasiquote `Defn` / `Init` / `Stat` trees internally; the change is the *boundary*. Each extension's public entry point now renders its `Defn`/`Init`/`Stat` output to a String via `DomainScalaParseBack.renderS30(_)` and returns `List[String]`/`String`; the `CogenProduct[T]` / `EnumProduct` / `AdtProduct` carriers expose four String splice slots — `defnAnyvalBases: List[String]` (prepend to `defn.templ.inits`), `companionCirceBases: List[String]` (prepend to `companionBase.templ.inits`), `companionCasts: List[String]` (append to `companionBase.templ.body.stats`), `siblings: List[String]` (top-level Defn fragments). At carrier render time, each String is parsed back via `DomainScalaParseBack.{parseInit, parseStat}` and spliced into the inner `Defn` outer shell; `ModuleTools.toSource` calls `Defn.syntax` once at the end. The net `scala.meta` reach is identical to M7 — the boundary moved from "per-call-site `prependBase` / `appendDefinitions`" to "per-extension `renderS30` + per-carrier `parseBack`". **Per-file LOC delta** (before → after): `products/CogenProduct.scala` 109 → 178; `products/RenderableCogenProduct.scala` 54 → 67; `domain/DomainScalaParseBack.scala` 75 → 87 (added `parseInit` + `parseStat` made public); `domain/DomainScalaTranslator.scala` 302 → 263 (per-emit fanout collapses now that splices route through slots); `domain/DomainInterfaceRenderer.scala` 242 → 246 (impl-struct extension chain switched to slot push, the special `[case class, structCirce trait, object]` order is now expressed via `siblings`); `domain/DomainServiceMethodProduct.scala` 543 → 540 (the `withCirce` / `withAdtCirce` helpers switch to slot push); `domain/extensions/DomainAnyvalExtension.scala` 153 → 168 (added `doModifyInit` to keep the renderer-internal `withAnyForStruct` / `withAnyForInterface` arms returning `List[Init]` for `InterfaceRenderer.mkTrait`'s direct `prependBase`); `domain/extensions/DomainCastSimilarExtension.scala` 163 → 168; `domain/extensions/DomainCastUpExtension.scala` 274 → 281 (added a private `generateUpcastsForImplStructInternal: List[Stat]` to keep the `Stat`-shape pipeline available for any future direct splicing — currently only the rendered-String public arm is consumed); `domain/extensions/DomainCastDownExpandExtension.scala` 128 → 130; `domain/extensions/DomainCirceTranslatorExtensionBase.scala` 505 → 521 (added `mkCirceTrait(ctx, name, defn, ownerId)` factory that renders BOTH the trait and the companion-base `Init` via `renderS30`; the `CirceTrait` case class now carries `name: String`, `defnText: String`, `initText: String` instead of `name: String`, `defn: Defn.Trait`). Total touched: ~530 LOC inserted, ~204 removed across 15 files. **Parse-back vocabulary added** — `DomainScalaParseBack.parseInit(src: String): Init` (wraps the source as `class _Probe extends $src`, parses via `Scala30`, extracts the single template init; `scala.meta` does not expose a top-level `parse[Init]` entry point). `parseStat` widened from `private` to package-visible so `CogenProductSplice` can call it for `companionCasts` and `siblings`. `DomainScalaParseBack` itself widened from `private[domain]` to `private[toscala]` so the `products` sibling package can call it. **Slot semantics**: every Slot defaults to `Nil`, so the no-extension-applied path is a zero-cost identity — `parseInit` / `parseStat` are not invoked when the slot is empty. Renderer-internal callers (`mkTrait` calling `prependBase` with `withAnyForStruct`-produced `Init`) keep the legacy `Init`-shape pipeline unchanged. **Test-suite touch-up**: 4 extension specs (`DomainCastDownExpandExtensionSpec`, `DomainCastSimilarExtensionSpec`, `DomainCastUpExtensionSpec`, `DomainCirceExtensionSpec`) drop the `scala.meta.dialects.Scala213(tree).syntax` rendering — the extensions now return rendered text directly; assertions consume `head` (cast helpers) / `defnText` (Circe trait) verbatim. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` byte-equal (every golden across the 28-domain corpus matches; the splice boundary preserves `Defn.syntax`-printed text because the parse-back roundtrip on extension-produced `Init`/`Stat` fragments is byte-stable — these fragments come from `q"..."` quasiquotes built under the Scala 3 parser dialect and re-printed via `dialect(tree).syntax` later, same as M5/M6/M7), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. **Stop conditions all clear**: parse-back vocabulary needed only one new entry (`parseInit`); the total LOC delta sits at ~530 (well under the 1000 ceiling); byte parity converged on round 1 (compile-error fix for `sibling` import + a stale `ScalaMetaTools._` import — neither was a parity defect). **Path to M8b (DomainAnyvalExtension → strings native)**: now that the extension entry points return `String`, the internal `q"..."` quasiquote → `renderS30(_)` step in `DomainAnyvalExtension.doModify` can be replaced with direct text composition (e.g. `s"$baseName"` for the AnyVal init) and `IRTGenerated.init()` → `s"$generatedQualifiedName"` for the renderer-internal `withAnyForStruct` arm — that drops the only scala.meta usage in the file. M8b lands as a focused 1-file diff (~150 LOC, no carrier change, no call-site change). Similar treatment scoped for M8c (CastSimilar / CastUp / CastDownExpand — these own the `q"implicit object Foo extends Cast[...] { override def convert(...) = $code }"` shape, which lowers cleanly to `s"..."` template literals once `DomainScalaStruct.fromFlat` consumers are inspected to confirm no further `Tree` interplay) and M8d (Circe — the deepest dependency, builds Encoder/Decoder traits with `Pat.Var` / `Term.Name` / `Lit.String` splices; needs careful indentation discipline given the Scala 3 indentation-sensitive parsing quirk M7 surfaced). M8e retires `DomainScalaParseBack` once the last consumer (Circe) goes String-native; M8f retires the remaining `scala.meta.*` imports from the Scala translator family.

- **PR-02 F-TextTree M7: port Scala service renderers off scala.meta** (2026-05-12) — Seventh milestone of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite; fourth cycle on the Scala renderer family. Migrates `DomainServiceRenderer` and `DomainServiceMethodProduct` from `scala.meta` quasiquotes onto `TextTree[ScalaRefHandle]` composition + `.mapRender(resolver.resolve)` at the renderer boundary. **Renderers ported** (LOC before → after): `DomainServiceRenderer.scala` 129 → 189; `DomainServiceMethodProduct.scala` 447 → 542. `DomainServiceContext.scala` unchanged at 82 (it carries small `scala.meta` Name/Type witnesses — `F.t`, `Ctx.t`, `Ctx.p`, `IO2.n`, `methodImport` — that the renderer lowers via `DomainScalaParseBack.renderS30(_)` at splice points; migrating those is M8 scope and would cascade into the small `Term.Name`/`Type.Name`/`tparam` API). **No foundation extensions required** — the witnesses M6 introduced (`TypeName`, `TypeFull`, `TermFull`, `TypeAbsolute`, `TypeFullWithin`, `TermFullWithin`) cover every service-renderer substitution shape.
