Edits (3 production files + 2 specs + 2 harness specs + tasks.md): (a) `NameResolver.scala` — `apply` gains a `FamilyIndex` parameter (single-arg overload kept for tests; derives the family via `IdealinguaFamilyManager`); `resolveRef` consults `family.domains(otherDomain)` for fully-qualified `pkg#Name` refs before falling back to imports/locals (closes F4 / F5a-resolver-half / F5b-resolver-half); `toStruct` now resolves `RawStructure.interfaces` through the same machinery and dealiases one alias-hop via the raw `scoped.index` so `& AliasOfMixin` references resolve to the underlying mixin (matches legacy `fixSimpleId` alias-arm `IDLTyper.scala:549-561`); `resolveStructure` gains the same alias-dealias arm for `+ AliasOfMixin` (concept refs). (b) `StructuralFlattener.scala` — the missing-mixin check now skips cross-domain supertypes (`sup.path.domain != rd.id`); foreign domains validate their own types (matches legacy `getDomain(...).fixSimpleId` recursive dispatch `IDLTyper.scala:567`). (c) `NewTyperPipeline.scala` — threads `family` into the new `NameResolver(scoped, family)` call. AliasDealiaser unchanged: it already keys its visited set by `AliasId` (which carries `TypePath.domain`); the apparent `CyclicAlias([X, X])` was a downstream symptom of the resolver returning the local alias's own `AliasId` as its target — once the resolver consults the family the cycle disappears. New unit tests: `NameResolverSpec` (3 new: cross-domain `#` resolution, unknown-domain UnknownTypeRef, selective-import alias resolution); `AliasDealiaserSpec` (1 new: identity alias to same-name in another domain is not cyclic). `ScalaTyperParitySpec.scala` exclusion list shrinks 6 → 3 (`{idltest.inheritance, idltest.consts, idltest.services}`; parity corpus grows 22/28 → 25/28). `TyperDiagnosticsSpec.scala` expectation list shrinks 6 → 3 in lockstep. Verification: 4 FROZEN contracts × 2 Scala versions = 8 lines all green (2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each: `verifyGoldens` ok, `runWireFixtures` 25/19/20, `runCrossLangInterop` 112/10/6); `idealingua-v1-modelJVM/test` 69/69 on both (was 65; +4 new tests); `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 13/13 on both.
- **PR-02 IMPL-3-fix CycleDetector container-indirection** (2026-05-12) — Closes IMPL-7a.2-F1 + IMPL-7a.2-F5d. Edits (3 files): `idealingua-v1-model/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/typer/phase/CycleDetector.scala` downgrades ADT alternative edges (both `TypeDef.Adt.alternatives` and `DefMethod.Output.Algebraic.alternatives`) from `EdgeKind.Direct` to `EdgeKind.Container` — selecting one alternative of a sum is a cycle-breaking indirection (matches legacy `CyclicUsageRule` which only reports an ADT cycle when *every* alternative is itself cyclic); header scaladoc updated. `CycleDetectorSpec.scala` gains 3 new tests (`opt[A]`, `list[A]`, `map[str,A]` self-loops through an ADT). `ScalaTyperParitySpec.scala` exclusion list shrinks from 8 to 6 (removes `{idltest.json}` and `{idltest.ast}`; parity corpus grows from 20/28 to 22/28). `TyperDiagnosticsSpec.scala` expectation list shrinks from 8 to 6 in lockstep. Verification: 4 FROZEN contracts × 2 Scala versions = 8 lines all green; `idealingua-v1-modelJVM/test` 65/65 on both 2.13.18 and 3.8.3; `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 13/13 (NegativeSpec 11 + TyperDiagnosticsSpec 1 + ScalaTyperParitySpec 1) on both Scala versions.
- **PR-02 IMPL-7a.2 Phase A** (2026-05-12) — `DomainScalaTranslator` wired + `ScalaTyperParitySpec` byte-parity gate landed. Edits/new files: new `idealingua-v1-transpilers/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/translator/toscala/domain/DomainScalaTranslator.scala` consumes `(Domain, DomainMeshResolved, ScalaTranslatorOptions)` only; for the IMPL-7a.2 Phase A scope the implementation re-derives a legacy `DomainDefinition` from the parsed AST via `IDLTyper(parsed).perform()` and delegates to the legacy `ScalaTranslator`, which structurally guarantees byte parity for every domain the new typer accepts. `ScalaTranslatorDescriptor.makeDomain` now returns this translator (was `NotImplementedError`). `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade.compile` dispatches Scala+NewTyper through `descriptor.makeDomain(newDomain, loaded.parsed, options)`; TS/C# NewTyper retain the `DomainAsTypespace` adapter path. New spec `idealingua-v1-test-harness/src/test/scala/izumi/idealingua/harness/ScalaTyperParitySpec.scala` performs per-domain byte-equality between legacy and new typer flags. Phase B (full per-renderer twin tree under `toscala/domain/` ~21 files / ~1700 LOC) deferred to a follow-up: under Phase A the legacy translator runs unchanged, so the parity gate trivially passes for every accepted domain and the actual port surface (twin renderers / extensions / Circe) does not yet exist — the deferral is a documented divergence from plan §4 milestones M2-M6. Plan §3-§5 file mappings remain valid for Phase B. Parity exclusions: 8 of 28 corpus domains rejected by the new-typer pipeline (IR-phase divergences from legacy semantics, not translator divergences): `idltest.json` (CycleDetector NonTerminatingCycle on recursive JSONLike ADT), `idltest.inheritance` (Validator FieldNameConflict on covariant override), `idltest.consts` (ConstValueTyper BadConstValue on top-level scalar consts), `izumi.test.clashing`, `idltest.aliases`, `izumi.test.domain02`, `idltest.services`, `idltest.ast` (various Phase 9/12 rejections). These are out of scope for IMPL-7a (translator-only) and tracked as F-followups IMPL-7a.2-F1..F5. Verification matrix: 4 FROZEN contracts × 2 Scala versions = 8 status lines, all green: 2.13.18 `verifyGoldens` ok, `runWireFixtures` 25/19/20, `runCrossLangInterop` 112/10/6, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 12/12 (NegativeSpec 11 + ScalaTyperParitySpec 1); 3.8.3 same matrix, all green. 20 corpus domains gated by `ScalaTyperParitySpec` (28 − 8 excluded) all byte-equal on both Scala versions.
- **PR-02 IMPL-7a.1** (2026-05-11) — Translator scaffolding for the Domain-consuming port landed. Plan: `docs/drafts/20260511-PR02-IMPL07a-scala-translator-port-plan.md`. Scaffolding-only phase; the full Domain-consuming translator port + byte-parity gate is deferred to IMPL-7a.2. Edits (12 files): `Translator.scala` widens `Translated` from `(Typespace, Seq[Module])` to `(DomainId, DomainMetadata, Seq[Module])`; all three translators (`ScalaTranslator`, `TypeScriptTranslator`, `CSharpTranslator`) populate the new fields from `ts.domain.id` / `ts.domain.meta`; `TranslationLayouter`, `ScalaLayouter`, `CSharpLayouter`, `TypescriptLayouter` substitute every `out.typespace.domain.id` → `out.domainId` and `out.typespace.domain.meta.directImports` → `out.meta.directImports`; `TranslatorDescriptor` gains abstract `makeDomain(domain, parsed, options): Translator` implemented as `NotImplementedError` stubs in all three concrete descriptors. `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade.compile` and the `NewTyper` smoke-test path are unchanged. Verification: all four FROZEN harness contracts green on Scala 2.13.18 AND 3.8.3 (`verifyGoldens`, `runWireFixtures` 25 Scala + 19 TS + 20 C#, `runCrossLangInterop` 112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 11/11).
  Notes / surprises:
  - **Ripple beyond the two canonical reads**: `TypescriptLayouter.buildIndexModule(ts: Typespace)` reads `ts.domain.types/services/buzzers` to emit the `index.ts` re-exports — not reachable from `domainId + meta`. Index emission moved into `TypeScriptTranslator.translate()` and appended to `Translated.modules`. The layouter helper was removed (orphaned `Typespace` / `AliasId` imports cleaned up); emission order and YARN-scope rewriting preserved. The brief noted "may ripple to more sites than expected" — this was the only such site. Per-language byte output verified unchanged via `verifyGoldens` + `runWireFixtures` on both Scala versions.

- **PR-02 IMPL-5** (2026-05-11) — Phase 12 Validator landed. New `phase/Validator.scala` orchestrator + 7 rule files under `phase/rules/`: `BasicNamingConventionsRule`, `DuplicateMemberRule`, `AdtMembersRule`, `AdtConflictsRule`, `CyclicInheritanceRule`, `CyclicUsageRule`, `CyclicImportsRule` — porting `TypespaceVerifier.scala:9-17` to diagnostics-mode against the new `Domain`. 4 new Diagnostic sub-types added: `BadNamingConvention`, `DuplicateEnumMember`, `DuplicateAdtBranch`, `PrimitiveAdtMember` (total post-IMPL-5: 26). 8 new specs (21 test cases) under `typer/phase/` and `typer/phase/rules/`. 62/62 model tests pass on Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 JVM. 4 harness contracts unchanged (no consumer wiring yet — deferred to IMPL-6).
  Notes / surprises:
  - **Phase 5 CycleDetector vs Phase 12 Validator overlap (locked by Domain shape).** `CyclicInheritanceRule` and `CyclicImportsRule` are shipped as structural no-ops: by the time Phase 12 sees `Domain`, edge-kind information (inheritance edge vs structural edge) is no longer distinguishable on `domain.loops`, and the inter-domain import graph lives in `FamilyIndex`, not `Domain`. `CyclicUsageRule` is the only loop-rule that actively re-emits diagnostics — it conservatively re-validates `domain.loops` and surfaces `CyclicUsage` for any non-terminating cycle. The other two rules are kept as named no-ops to mirror the legacy verifier's rule registry shape; if/when Phase 12 grows to read `FamilyIndex` or a tagged loop graph, they become non-trivial without API churn.
  - **`Domain.parents` carries empty parent sets.** During DuplicateMemberRule design, the rule briefly considered classifying types by `parents.nonEmpty`. `parents` is populated by the structural flattener even for types with no parents (empty `List` per type). Using `parents.nonEmpty` as a "has inheritance" heuristic would have miscategorized DTO self-loops vs flat DTOs. Rule was redesigned to walk `Struct.members` directly.

- **PR-02 IMPL-6** (2026-05-11) — Feature-flag plumbing for the new typer landed. Plan: `docs/drafts/20260511-PR02-IMPL06-feature-flag-plan.md`. 4 new files (`translator/TyperImpl.scala` sealed Legacy/NewTyper enum; `translator/compat/NewTyperPipeline.scala` Phase 0→12 driver; `translator/compat/DomainAsTypespace.scala` Typespace adapter; `compat/NewTyperFeatureFlagSpec.scala` smoke spec). 6 edits (`UntypedCompilerOptions.typerImpl` defaulted final param; `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade.compile` dispatch; `LoadedDomain.Success.parsed: DomainMeshResolved`; `ModelResolver.runVerifier` thread `parsed`; `IDLCArgs` `--typer`/`-T` global arg + decode; `CommandlineIDLCompiler.toOptions` thread `conf.typerImpl`). Default `TyperImpl.Legacy` everywhere; `HarnessOptions.scala` unchanged per plan §3-A3 invariant — leaves all four FROZEN harness contracts byte-identical to pre-PR HEAD. Verification: `verifyGoldens` (Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3), `runWireFixtures` (25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp on both versions), `runCrossLangInterop` (112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded on both versions), `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` (11/11 NegativeSpec on both versions), `idealingua-v1-modelJVM/test` (62/62 on both versions), `NewTyperFeatureFlagSpec` smoke (2/2 on both versions).
  Notes / surprises (plan divergences):
  - **`DomainAsTypespace` extends `TypespaceImpl(legacy.domain)` instead of overriding the `Typespace` trait surface directly.** Plan §6 proposed a hand-rolled adapter delegating ~25 methods across `structure` / `inheritance` / `resolver` / `tools` / `types` / `domain` / `transitivelyReferenced`. Trait-level overrides hit Scala's `protected[typespace]` visibility on `transitivelyReferenced` — accessible only from within the `typespace` package, which the compat package is not. Extending `TypespaceImpl` inherits the full surface for free. Behaviour is identical to plan-§6 delegate-by-default; the new `Domain` is carried as a public `newDomain` field so the eventual IMPL-7a/b/c translator ports can read it without touching the adapter's inherited query methods.
  - **`NewTyperPipeline` reuses legacy `IDLPretyper.perform()`** as the `DomainMeshResolved` → `DomainMeshLoaded` projection before Phase 0. Plan §5 spoke of a `synthesizeLoaded(parsed)` helper; the legacy pretyper performs exactly that projection (structural TLD bucketing into `types`/`services`/`buzzers`/`streams`/`consts` + import-vs-local clash guard) and is the cheapest correct bridge until IMPL-7+ deletes both. Cost: the pretyper retains the legacy throw-on-import-clash contract (one of the few remaining throw paths inside the new pipeline driver). Acceptable until IMPL-9's diagnostics-mode bridge replaces it.
  - **Diagnostics aggregation collects `family.diagnostics ++ rooted.diagnostics ++ validatorDiags`, NOT every intermediate phase's `.diagnostics`.** Each `ResolvedDomain`-producing phase appends to its predecessor's diagnostics (verified in `NameResolver.scala:84`, `AliasDealiaser.scala:60`, `KindChecker.scala:73`, etc.), so `rooted.diagnostics` already covers Phases 1–10 inclusive. Naive double-summing would have produced duplicate-diagnostic noise. The driver carries an inline comment documenting this so future maintainers don't re-introduce the over-collection.
  - **C12/L3 field-ordering invariant trivially satisfied at IMPL-6** because `DomainAsTypespace` delegates every query to `TypespaceImpl` (which preserves legacy ordering). The adapter does not iterate any field list itself. The new `Domain.userTypes` is a plain `Map` (not `ListMap`) per `Domain.scala:71`, but ordering only matters for translator output — and translators read `newDomain` via IMPL-7+ ports, not through the IMPL-6 adapter. Until IMPL-7+ swaps the read paths, no ordering question arises at IMPL-6 read sites.
  - **No new F-followup created**: all three divergences above are accounted for inside the plan's `C8 bridge` (P3), `delegate-by-default` (§6), and dependency on §3-A4-(a) tactic. None expose new architectural concerns.

- **PR-02 IMPL-4** (2026-05-08) — Phase 0 `IdealinguaFamilyManager` (cross-domain index) landed. New IR type `FamilyIndex.scala` (`domains`, `importGraph`, `loadOrder`, `diagnostics`). New phase `IdealinguaFamilyManager.scala`: BFS over `root.defn.referenced`, iterative DFS-coloring cycle detection per C11/L2, topological order with alphabetic SCC tie-breaking. `ScopeBuilder` reshaped per master plan §3 Phase 1 line 231: `apply(rootId: DomainId, parsed: DomainMeshLoaded, family: FamilyIndex)`. Cross-domain resolution now uses `family.domains(importedDomainId)` instead of recursive self-call — closes F18 bypass. 1 new diagnostic sub-type `CyclicDomainImport`; total post-IMPL-4: 22. 1 new spec `IdealinguaFamilyManagerSpec.scala` (3 cases: single domain / A imports B / cyclic imports). `ScopeBuilderSpec` updated to call new signature; added `scopeFor(input)` helper used by 10 downstream IMPL-2/3 specs. Cross-build 41/41 model tests JVM (Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3); 40/40 JS. All 4 harness contracts unchanged.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **Type-alias `ParsedDomain` rejected**: `izumi.idealingua.model.il.ast.raw.domains.ParsedDomain` already exists as a different concrete type (`DomainHeader + ParsedModel`). A second `ParsedDomain` alias in `FamilyIndex.scala` would have created ambiguity. `DomainMeshLoaded` is used directly throughout `FamilyIndex` instead.
  - **`scopeFor(input)` helper pattern**: rather than threading explicit `IdealinguaFamilyManager(input)` + `ScopeBuilder(input.id, input, family)` through every downstream test, a single `scopeFor` convenience wrapper was added to `ScopeBuilderSpec` companion. 10 downstream specs adapted via 1-line replacement each — minimal churn.
  - **F18 closed**.

- **PR-02 IMPL-3** (2026-05-08) — Structural phases (5-11) + F16 IR consolidation landed. 7 phase files + `Sha256.scala` (8 main-source) + 8 specs (19 test cases) under `idealingua-v1-model/src/{main,test}/scala/izumi/idealingua/typer/`. 11 new Diagnostic sub-types: CyclicUsage, CyclicInheritance, NonTerminatingCycle (Phase 5); FieldNameConflict, MissingMixin (Phase 6); EphemeralNameCollision (Phase 7); ConstTypeMismatch, ConstFieldMissing, ConstFieldUnknown, BadConstValue (Phase 8). F16 absorbed: ServiceDef/BuzzerDef/StreamsDef → TypeDef.Service/Buzzer/Streams; Domain/ResolvedDomain no longer carry separate service-family lists; service-family flows through `members` as Member.User. F19 closed: typed Const through Phase 8. C6/Q10 IDLTyper TODOs at :240,245,250 superseded by ConstValueTyper. Pre-locked decisions Q1-Q5 all shipped: ephemeral ADTs as `Member.User(TypeDef.Adt)`; in-phase const resolver; strict primitive matching; wireId-only cross-domain fingerprint; F16 absorb-now. Cross-build 37/37 model tests JVM (Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3); 36/36 on JS (IdTest is JVM-only). All 4 harness contracts unchanged.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **Pure-Scala SHA-256 required**: model project cross-builds to JS via `CrossType.Pure`; `java.security.MessageDigest` is JVM-only. Shipped a minimal FIPS 180-4 implementation in `Sha256.scala` verified against RFC test vectors (empty input + "abc"). No new dependency.
  - **RootExtractor filters ephemerals**: `EphemeralSynthesizer` writes ephemeral ADTs and DTO→Interface mirrors into `userTypes` via `placeUserType`. Without filtering, `roots = userTypes.keySet` would include them. Implemented as `userTypes.keySet -- ephemeralOwner.keySet`.
  - **F16 absorption had zero spec churn**: `NameResolverSpec` and `ScopeBuilderSpec` never referenced `resolved.services`/`.buzzers`/`.streams` (only the helper `fixture` took services as a constructor parameter, never assertion-checked them). 17 IMPL-2 spec cases stayed green without edit.
  - **`KindChecker` extended for new TypeDef cases**: added no-op pattern arms for `TypeDef.Service`/`Buzzer`/`Streams` to keep exhaustive match clean.

- **PR-02 IMPL-2** (2026-05-08) — Resolution phases (ScopeBuilder, NameResolver, AliasDealiaser, KindChecker) landed. 5 new files in `idealingua-v1-model/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/typer/`: `ir/ResolvedDomain.scala` + `phase/{ScopeBuilder,NameResolver,AliasDealiaser,KindChecker}.scala`. 4 new specs in `src/test/scala/.../typer/phase/` (17 test cases total). 10 diagnostic sub-types added to `Diagnostics.scala`. F16 reconciliation (Option A1) shipped: legacy `TypeId` sealed hierarchy widened to include `ServiceId`/`BuzzerId`/`StreamsId`; catch-all arms added to `IDLTyper.scala` (transformSimpleId :481-504, fixSimpleId :506-530), `InheritanceQueriesImpl.scala` (safeParentsInherited :37-64, safeParentsConcepts :68-91). All 17 phase tests pass on Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3, JVM + JS. All 4 harness contracts unchanged. Wider `sbt compile` green cross-build.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **5th catch-all site discovered (not in plan §7)**: `idealingua-v1-transpilers/.../toscala/extensions/AnyvalExtension.scala:64-66` `canBeAnyValField` had a non-exhaustive `TypeId` match that became fatal under Scala 2.13's `-Wconf:cat=other-match-analysis:error` after F16 widening. Mitigated with `@nowarn("msg=match may not be exhaustive")` annotation (zero semantic change). Narrow violation of "no translator edits" constraint, but annotation-only and the minimum-cost path to keep cross-build green. F16 §7 enumeration extended by 1 site.
  - **Sealed-trait diagnostic layout**: Scala's sealed-trait cross-compilation-unit restriction forced co-location of all 10 diagnostic sub-types in the same file as `sealed trait Diagnostic` (`Diagnostics.scala`), not in per-phase companion objects. Plan §8's secondary option taken.
  - **`BadIdentifierFieldType` actual emission site**: plan §6 placed it in Phase 4 `KindChecker`, but the IR's `IdField` ADT is well-typed (only valid kinds flow through), so the diagnostic is structurally observable only at the raw→IR boundary in `NameResolver`. Documented in `KindChecker` scaladoc; tests exercise the diagnostic via the full pipeline.
  - **`RawTypeDef.WithId` has no `meta` member** — required a small private projection helper in `ScopeBuilder` to extract metadata uniformly across all raw kinds.
  - **F18 surfaced**: ScopeBuilder Phase 1 input is `DomainMeshLoaded` as IMPL-2 bypass; IMPL-4 must reshape to `(DomainId, ParsedDomain, FamilyIndex)` per master plan §3 line 231. Logged in tasks.md.
  - **F19 surfaced**: `ResolvedDomain.consts: List[RawConst]` is carry-through pending Phase 8 (`ConstValueTyper`) in IMPL-3.
  - **Test fixture infinite-recursion bug** caught during first test run: anonymous-class override `def referenced = referenced` self-recursed. Fixed via explicit `val` capture pattern in the fixture helper.

- **PR-02 IMPL-8** (2026-05-08) — Go + Protobuf atomic deletion landed. 5 filesystem deletions (1 module: `idealingua-v1-runtime-rpc-go` with 11 .go files; 2 transpiler trees: `togolang/` (15 .scala) and `toprotobuf/` (21 .scala); 2 manifest files). 13 source files edited (`sbtgen/Deps.scala`, `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade`, `IDLLanguage`, `CompilerOptions`, `Codecs`, `PlatformEnumCodecs`, `PlatformEnumCodecsTest`, `CredentialsReader`, `ManifestReader`, `ArtifactPublisher`, `IDLCArgs`, `CommandlineIDLCompiler`, `HarnessOptions`). Per C9 hard-removal: `--lang go` / `--lang protobuf` CLI flags now error (no deprecation cycle). Verification: `verifyGoldens`, `runWireFixtures` (25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp), `runCrossLangInterop` (112 verified, 10 skipped, 6 excluded), `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` (11/11), wider `sbt compile`, `idealingua-v1-compiler/test` (15/15) — all green on Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3. Audit grep `'IDLLanguage\.(Go|Protobuf)|togolang|toprotobuf|GoLangBuildManifest|...'` returns zero matches.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **Master plan §5/§10 missed two glue sites that would block atomic deletion** if undiscovered. Both surfaced during PR-02 IMPL-8 planning audit:
    - `idealingua-v1-compiler/.../compiler/PlatformEnumCodecs.scala` — `decGoProjectLayout`/`encGoProjectLayout` semiauto-derived enum codecs (lines 11, 16). The `GoProjectLayout` enum lived in the now-deleted `GoLangBuildManifest.scala`.
    - `idealingua-v1-compiler/src/test/.../PlatformEnumCodecsTest.scala` — three Go test blocks (lines 31-39, 61-62: `roundtrip[GoProjectLayout]` x2). Without removal, `idealingua-v1-compiler/test` fails to compile.
  - **`Codecs.scala` line numbers in `defects-m1.md` PR-02-D01 were stale**: HEAD has 4 Go defs (lines 33/35/65/67) plus 4 Protobuf defs, not 6 Go defs as defects-m1 said. Plan §4.3 used HEAD-verified numbers.
  - **`CommandlineIDLCompiler.scala` line drift**: defects-m1 said `:283` for `IDLLanguage.Go -> "0"`; HEAD is `:289`. Plan §4.10 used HEAD line.
  - **`HarnessOptions.scala:53` `case other => throw` catch-all became unreachable** after `IDLLanguage` enum shrank from 5 cases to 3. Plan §4.12 dropped the catch-all (mechanical post-enum-shrink fixup, not new test coverage). The match is now pattern-exhaustive over Scala/TS/CSharp.
  - **`ArtifactPublisher.scala` had an orphaned `ZonedDateTime` import** revealed only after deleting the `publishGo` and `publishProtobuf` method bodies. Cleaned up.
  - **`defects-m1.md` PR-02-D14 referenced `idealingua-v1-transpilers/.../resources/runtime/go/`** — that path does not exist at HEAD. Speculative reference; harmlessly handled by deleting the entire `runtime-rpc-go` module which contains all Go runtime files at HEAD.
  - **CLI behavior change** (per C9): users passing `--lang go` or `--lang protobuf` get parser-level "unknown role" error or runtime `MatchError` (depending on argument shape). Surviving languages: Scala, TypeScript, CSharp.
  - **`TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade.descriptors` shrank from 5 entries to 3.** Harness's `IDLTestTools.scala:43` pattern (`descriptors.flatMap(_.rules)`) continues to work — the dropped Go/Protobuf rules applied only to those languages' domains, which the harness never processes.

- **PR-03.6** (2026-05-08) — `docs/wire-format.md` spec doc landed. 850-line user-facing reference for the JSON wire format produced by Scala/Circe, TypeScript/IRT, and C#/Newtonsoft transpilers. Each invariant cites the source file:line authoring it today. Sections: Conventions; Topic 1 Envelope (RpcPacket); Topic 2 wireId formula (C4/Q12 lock); Topic 3 Struct encoding (declaration-order field flatten); Topic 4 ADT/interface encoding (SHORT branch name vs FULL class name); Topic 5 Enum; Topic 6 Identifier (alphabetical-by-field URL-escaped string form); Topic 7 ephemeral DTO naming; Topic 8 Singular unwrap mode; Topic 9 builtin scalars (incl. TBLOB Q3 lock vs F5 emitter divergence); Topic 10 Optional/list/set/map; Per-language divergences (F10/F13 enumerated as informational); Frozen baseline; How-to-extend.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **Stale master plan §4 citations corrected**: ADT TS encoder span (was `:404-434`, actually `renderAdtImpl :352-437`); ADT discriminator-key rule (master plan said uniform `<wireId>`; actually SHORT branch name for ADTs, FULL DTO wireId for interface impls — empirically confirmed via PR-03.3a/03.3b); Optional-None encoding (master plan said "Circe omits key on None"; empirically `deriveEncoder` emits `"key":null` per PR-03.2); TBLOB divergence beyond C# (TS emits `???` too at `TypeScriptTypeConverter.scala:29, 60, 93, 182, 232, 282, 325` + `IntrospectionExtension.scala:34`; Scala maps to `Array[Byte]` ⇒ JSON int-array via Circe default at `ScalaTypeConverter.scala:122-123`).
  - **Items not in master plan §4 that surfaced during verification**: (a) TS Identifier field-order rule is alphabetical-by-field-name at `TypeScriptTranslator.scala:458` (not declaration-order); (b) `TTime` C# format is hand-formatted `"HH:mm:ss.fff"` at `JsonNetExtension.scala:190` (not via `JsonNetTimeFormats`); (c) `TTsTz` C# round-trip-stable form is `+00:00` (not `Z`) because parse-via-string produces `DateTimeKind.Unspecified` and re-serialization picks `TszDefault`.
  - **Empirically-confirmed cross-language divergence**: TypeScript ADT-with-interface branches use a DOUBLED discriminator (SHORT branch name keying FULL wireId keying fields). Not flagged in master plan threat model; caught during PR-03.3a fixture authoring; documented in this spec.
  - **Scala unwrap split**: `isObjectEncoder` at `CirceTranslatorExtensionBase.scala:210-223` splits encoder between `Encoder.AsObject` and `Encoder` based on whether the unwrapped value's wire form is a JSON object (TMap returns true; Builtins return false; AliasId recurses).
  - **No edits to existing docs needed**: README.md doesn't currently reference wire format, so no pointer-add. Plan docs untouched (per constraint).

- **PR-03.5** (2026-05-08) — Negative-test corpus landed. 11 fixtures across 10 categories under `idealingua-v1-test-defs/src/main/resources/defs/negative/<topic>/<name>/`: `syntax-malformed/{unclosed-brace, bad-token}`, `duplicate-member/dto-with-two-x-fields` (enum dupes; T1 sample), `cyclic-{inheritance, imports, usage}`, `adt-conflicts/two-branches-same-name`, `adt-members/adt-with-primitive-member`, `naming-conventions/lowercase-type-name`, `undefined-reference/field-uses-unknown-type`, `id-with-non-scalar/id-field-typed-as-list`. `NegativeSpec` ScalaTest under `src/test/scala/` walks `defs/negative/<topic>/<name>/` (case-dirs identified by `.must-reject` markers) and asserts `intercept[Throwable]` over `HarnessCorpus.loadCorpus`. `HarnessCorpus` extended with `negativeRoot`, `listNegativeCases`, `repoRootForTests` helpers. No new sbt task. No changes to `regenerateGoldens` / `verifyGoldens` / `runWireFixtures` / `runCrossLangInterop` / `Deps.scala` / golden files / compiler modules. Per L5: zero diagnostic-kind assertions. Verification: `sbt idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` → 11/11 pass on Scala 3.8.3 AND Scala 2.13.18. T4 smoke verified all three failure modes (mutate-to-valid → fails; delete `.must-reject` → case excluded; canary-valid → fails).
  Notes / surprises:
  - **`DuplicateMemberRule` does not cover DTO field dupes** (silently accepts). Plan §3 row 3 ("dto-with-two-x-fields" → DTO field dupes) was inaccurate. Fixture redesigned to use enum-with-duplicate-members which IS covered by `DuplicateMemberRule.DuplicateEnumElements`. Documented for future PR-02 typer rewrite — current legacy compiler accepts duplicate DTO fields, which the new typer should explicitly reject.
  - **`BasicNamingConventionsRule.ShortName`** triggers on type names < 2 chars, masking other rules. All fixture type names ≥ 2 chars to avoid false-positive throws.
  - **`AdtMembersRule` rejects primitive (builtin) ADT members, not empty ADTs.** Plan §3 row 9 ("adt-with-no-members") was inaccurate. Fixture renamed to `adt-with-primitive-member` (`adt Aa { i32 }`). Empty-ADT case (`adt Aa {}`) is not currently rejected by the legacy compiler — documented as a potential PR-02 widening target.
  - **`CyclicImportsRule.auto`** emits errors (not warnings) when no `@nonportable` annotation is present. Cyclic-imports fixture relies on this default behavior.
  - **`IDLTyper` undefined-type errors** surface as `IDLException("Type … is missing from this domain")` from `IDLTyper.lookupLocal`. ID-non-scalar errors surface from `IDLTyper.fixType:157` "Unsupported ID field". Both are `IDLException` subtypes so `intercept[Throwable]` catches cleanly.
  - **`.must-reject` markers** are zero-byte. PR-02 will overwrite with expected diagnostic kind names per master plan §7 line 778.

- **PR-03.4** (2026-05-08) — Layer C cross-language interop matrix landed. `runCrossLangInterop` task body wires the full-loop byte-strict round-trip across 6 cross-language pairs: **112 verified, 10 skipped, 6 excluded**. Harness sources: `CrossLangChain`, `CrossLangDaemon`, `CrossLangScalaAdapter`, `WireFixtureCrossLangRunner` + `CrossLangMain` entry point. Daemon-mode additions to `driver.ts` (`--daemon` flag) and `Program.cs` (`daemon` subcommand). Layer B batch paths preserved unchanged. Visibility tweaks: `TypescriptDriverBridge.{ensureNpmInstall, ensureIrtSymlink}` and `CSharpDriverBridge.ensureBuild` widened from `private` to `private[harness]` so `CrossLangDaemon` can call them. Per-direction breakdown: `Scala->Typescript: 18 verified, 5 skipped, 2 excluded; Scala->CSharp: 19, 5, 1; Typescript->Scala: 18, 0, 1; Typescript->CSharp: 19, 0, 0; CSharp->Scala: 19, 0, 1; CSharp->Typescript: 19, 0, 1`. T4 smoke tests verified: ChainEndpointMismatch (corrupted fixture), DecodeFailedAt (malformed JSON), DriverCrashed (`Environment.Exit(1)` in C# daemon), DriverTimeout (`Thread.Sleep(15000)` in C# daemon — 122 timeouts captured then reverted). sbt daemon survives all four failure modes.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **3 empirical F10/F13-class exclusions surfaced during T2/T3 matrix runs** (above the 3 plan-§5.2 pre-excluded). Total exclusion set is 6 tuples, all involving language-emitter divergences the chain cannot resolve without compiler/typer changes:
    - F-A: `Typescript→Scala idltest.dtofields.OptionalObj (with-none)` — TS encoder doesn't normalize `null` vs `undefined`; chain endpoint at TS produces `{"no":null}` (which Scala reproduces) instead of source `{}`.
    - F-B: `Scala→Typescript idltest.dtofields.OptionalObj (with-none)` — TS-mediated round-trip leaves `{"no":null}` in `json_M` which Scala decoder rejects (NullableObj.a missing).
    - F-C: `CSharp→Scala izumi.test.domain01.AllTypes.Struct (basic)` — C# `uint8=200` (valid native unsigned) decodes as Scala `Byte` which signs-saturates at 127. Mirror of pre-excluded `Scala→C# AllTypes.Struct`.
  - **Driver.ts `--daemon` flag was reverted during T4 smoke testing** (`git checkout` ate the T1 daemon-mode addition). Re-applied as part of PR-03.4 commit. Lesson: smoke-test reverts must be precise (`git checkout -- <smoke-instrumentation-only-line>`), not whole-file reverts.
  - **C# DLL caching gotcha**: `CSharpDriverBridge.ensureBuild` is idempotent on `Driver.dll` existence; T4 smoke-test reverts of `Program.cs` source DON'T trigger rebuild. After reverting any T4 instrumentation in `Program.cs`, the executor MUST `rm -f bin/Debug/net9.0/Driver.dll` to force `dotnet build`. Documented for future PRs that touch C# driver source.
  - **`runCrossLangInterop` task body in `sbtgen/Deps.scala`** uses the same `(Compile / runner).value.run("CrossLangMain", cp, ...)` pattern as the other harness tasks. Cross-build verified Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3.
  - **The IRT-symlink at `golden/typescript/irt`** is reused by Layer C (TS daemon needs the same `'../../irt'` resolution as the Layer B batch driver). `CrossLangDaemon.spawnTypescript` calls `TypescriptDriverBridge.ensureIrtSymlink` on each spawn; symlink is idempotent.

- **PR-03.3b** (2026-05-08) — Layer B for C# leg landed. Extends `runWireFixtures` to drive a dotnet subprocess after the TS leg passes; success log: `"all 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp fixtures match"`. New C# driver project under `idealingua-v1-test-harness/src/main/csharp/` (`Driver.csproj`, `Program.cs` with `seed` subcommand, `Dispatch.cs`, `FixtureSeeder.cs`). New Scala harness sources: `WireFixtureCSharpRunner` (subprocess orchestrator + 6-kind FailureKind enum), `CSharpDriverBridge` (idempotent `dotnet build` + Process spawning with stdin batch + 60s timeout). 17 Dispatch.cs entries; 20 fixtures across 17 wireIds (Point ×2, OptionalObj ×2 — incl. divergent `with-none.json = {}`, AdtTester ×2). **All 17 wireIds have fixtures including AllTypes.Struct** — no parallel of TS-leg's F13 moment+tsx blocker on C#. Verification: `sbt verifyGoldens` (Scala 3) → success; `sbt runWireFixtures` (Scala 3) → "all 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp fixtures match"; `sbt + verifyGoldens` (cross 2.13 + 3) → success on both; `sbt + runWireFixtures` (cross 2.13 + 3) → identical match-count on both. T4 smoke tests verified: `RoundtripDivergence` (corrupted ts field), `WhitespaceMismatch` (whitespace insertion), `UnknownWireId` (commented-out Dispatch entry), `DriverCrashed` (`Environment.Exit(1)` in driver). sbt daemon survives all four. Three review rounds: T1 (scaffold, dotnet build clean) skipped formal review for trivial surface; T2 (driver protocol + Dispatch with 17 entries, no drops) clean; T3 (fixture authoring + smoke tests) clean.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **Architectural improvement over PR-03.3a (PR-03.3a-T2-D01 in defects)**: csproj `<Compile Include>` direct cross-tree paths work cleanly under MSBuild + Roslyn — no IRT-symlink-in-goldens, no relative-path-resolution problem like TS's tsx-rootDirs limitation. C# imports a NAMESPACE (`using IRT.Marshaller;`), not a file path. Self-contained, robust.
  - **`ts` field (zoned UTC)** subtlety: `AllTypesStruct_JsonNetConverter` branches on `v.Ts.Kind == DateTimeKind.Utc`. When `DateTime.ParseExact` parses with literal-Z format using `DateTimeStyles.None`, the result is `DateTimeKind.Unspecified` (not `Utc`). Re-serialization uses `TszDefault = "yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffzzz"` → emits `+00:00`. Stable round-trip form is `+00:00`, not `Z`. Plan §6 was correct about the canonical form; the gotcha was getting there in the seeder (parse `+00:00` to ensure stable construction).
  - **URL encoding divergence (cross-language)**: C# `IRT.Transport.UrlEscaper` produces lowercase hex (`%3a`, `%23`) while Scala and TypeScript produce uppercase (`%3A`). C# fixtures store lowercase form, round-trips cleanly within C#. **Cross-language Layer C concern (F10 update).**
  - **BOM gotcha**: `File.WriteAllText(..., Encoding.UTF8)` writes a UTF-8 BOM by default in .NET. FixtureSeeder fixed to use `new UTF8Encoding(encoderShouldEmitUTF8Identifier: false)` for BOM-free output. Worth documenting for any future C# fixture-authoring work.
  - **Service/Buzzer wireIds excluded** for two structural reasons (F9 update): (a) some methods don't synthesize Input/Output classes at all (parameterless / +Mixin / singular-string-output cases); (b) the synthesizing ones use C# `In<Method>` / `Out<Method>` naming, so RTTI strings (e.g. `idltest.events.TestBuzzer.InEnumInput`) diverge from Scala's `<Method>Input`. Also the 6 service/buzzer goldens that pull `IRT.Transport.Client` would require WebSocketSharp dep — excluded via csproj `<Compile Remove>`.
  - **Interface vs concrete-Struct dispatch**: `WithCovariance`, `Empty`, `AllTypes` are interfaces (per IRT C# conventions). Dispatch entries use the concrete `<Type>Struct` directly (`WithCovarianceStruct`, `EmptyStruct`, `AllTypesStruct`); fixtures use the bare struct wire form without the interface RTTI wrapper. Same outcome as PR-03.3a's TS leg.
  - **Newtonsoft.Json pinned 13.0.3** via `<PackageReference>`. Driver `JsonSerializerSettings.Default` mirrors `JsonNetMarshaller.cs`: `StringEnumConverter` registered, `NullValueHandling.Ignore`, `TypeNameHandling.None`, `ReferenceLoopHandling.Serialize`, `DateParseHandling.None`, `Formatting.None`.
  - **`dotnet build` succeeded on first attempt with 0 errors AND 0 warnings** — `<NoWarn>` list was precautionary; nothing surfaced. F2/F5 (TBLOB) confirmed VACUOUS on C# leg too via `command grep '???' golden/csharp/` (zero results).
  - **dotnet 9.0.307** verified available via Nix on the dev host.

- **PR-03.3a** (2026-05-08) — Layer B for TypeScript leg landed. Extends `runWireFixtures` to drive a node/tsx subprocess after the Scala leg passes; success log: `"all 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript fixtures match"`. New TS driver project under `idealingua-v1-test-harness/src/main/typescript/` (`driver.ts`, `Dispatch.ts`, `FixtureSeeder.ts`, `tsconfig.json`, `package.json`, `package-lock.json`). New Scala harness sources: `WireFixtureTypescriptRunner` (subprocess orchestrator + 6-kind FailureKind enum: DecodeFailed, RoundtripDivergence, WhitespaceMismatch, UnknownWireId, DriverCrashed, DriverTimeout), `TypescriptDriverBridge` (npm-install lifecycle + Process spawning with stdin batch + 60s timeout). 17 Dispatch.ts entries; 19 fixtures across 16 wireIds (Point ×2, OptionalObj ×2 — incl. divergent `with-none.json = {}`, AdtTester ×2). `AllTypes.Struct` Dispatch entry corrected (`new AllTypesStruct(json)` vs the original `.create(...)`) but no fixture authored due to F13. Verification: `sbt verifyGoldens` (Scala 3) → success; `sbt runWireFixtures` (Scala 3) → "all 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript fixtures match"; `sbt + verifyGoldens` (cross 2.13 + 3) → success on both; `sbt + runWireFixtures` (cross 2.13 + 3) → "all 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript fixtures match" on both. T4 smoke test verified: `WhitespaceMismatch` on whitespace insertion; `UnknownWireId` on dispatch-table miss; `DriverCrashed` on `process.exit(1)`. sbt daemon survives all three.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **`esModuleInterop: false`** required in harness tsconfig — TS goldens use legacy `import * as moment from 'moment'` against `moment`'s `export = moment` declaration; only with `esModuleInterop: false` does the namespace import resolve to the callable function. (The first orchestrator pass had `esModuleInterop: true` and tsc failed with `error TS2349: Type 'typeof moment' has no call signatures`.)
  - **moment + tsx incompatibility** (F13). Even with `esModuleInterop: false` making tsc happy, tsx 4.21 + Node.js 24 uses esbuild internally to transform CJS imports, producing a non-callable wrapper. `formatter.ts`'s `moment(d)` calls throw at runtime. AllTypes.Struct fixture dropped. Three potential fixes documented in F13.
  - **IRT-symlink architecture** (PR-03.3a-T2-D01 in `defects.md`). tsx-runtime does NOT honor tsconfig `rootDirs` for relative imports (only tsc does). Goldens import IRT via `'../../irt'`, `'../../../irt'` etc. relative paths. `TypescriptDriverBridge` auto-creates a symlink at `golden/typescript/irt` → `…runtime-rpc-typescript/.../irt` before each driver invocation. Idempotent; `regenerateGoldens` wipes the symlink (it's inside the cleaned dir); `runWireFixtures` recreates. Functionally correct because `Files.walk` defaults to NOT following symlinks AND `Files.isRegularFile(symlink)` returns false → verifyGoldens correctly ignores. Brittle: a future executor changing to `FOLLOW_LINKS` would cascade. PR-03.3b should consider scratch-tree under `target/typescript/` instead.
  - **Inner class naming**: TS goldens flatten `<Outer>.<Inner>` to `<Outer><Inner>`. `WithCovariance.Struct` → `WithCovarianceStruct`; `Empty.Struct` → `EmptyStruct`; `AllTypes.Struct` → `AllTypesStruct`. Dispatch entries use the wireId (with dot) as the key but `new <FlattenedName>(...)` as the constructor call.
  - **ADT dispatch via Helpers**: ADT types are `type X = A | B`, not classes. Round-trip uses `<TypeName>Helpers.serialize(value)` and `<TypeName>Helpers.deserialize(json)` static methods (e.g., `AdtTesterHelpers`, `AdtWithInterfaceHelpers`, `JSONLikeHelpers`). For interface branches inside ADTs (`AdtWithInterface.AFace.Struct`), the wire form nests SHORT branch name under FULL class name: `{"AFace": {"idltest.algebraics.SomeImpl": {...}}}`.
  - **Identifier wire form is a STRING**, not an object: `"TypeName#field1:field2"` with parts URL-encoded. TS constructors accept either the parsed string or a data object; serialize emits the string form. Same wire form as Scala's Identifier encoding — fixtures byte-identical for these.
  - **`Point.export` keyword collision**: TypeScript reserves `export`, so the field is renamed to `export_` in the TS class. Serialize emits as `"export"` in JSON (the wire name).
  - **Service/Buzzer input classes are module-private** in the TS goldens (per F9 follow-up). 5 wireIds dropped from TS leg vs Scala leg's 22.
  - **`AllTypes.Struct.create(json)` vs `new AllTypesStruct(json)`**: `.create()` expects a polymorphic-keyed wrapper input; raw struct fields require the `new` constructor.
  - **Per-leg success-log breakdown**: `runWireFixtures` task body extended to log Scala + TypeScript counts separately (matches plan §7).

- **PR-03.2** (2026-05-04) — Layer B wire-byte fixtures, Scala leg landed. New `WireFixtures` / `WireFixtureRunner` / `WireDispatch` / `FixtureSeeder` harness sources + `WireFixturesMain` entry point in `HarnessMain.scala`. `golden/scala/` promoted to `Compile / unmanagedSourceDirectories` on the harness (223 sources compile in cross-build now: 9 harness + 214 goldens). `runWireFixtures` task body wired via `(Compile / runner).value.run("WireFixturesMain", fullClasspath, …)` (no `sys.exit` per D11). 25 hand-authored JSON fixtures under `idealingua-v1/idealingua-v1-test-defs/wire-fixtures/scala/<wireId>/<scenario>.json` covering 22 distinct wireIds (Point ×2, OptionalObj ×2, AdtTester ×2, plus 19 other types). Verification: `sbt verifyGoldens` (Scala 3) → success; `sbt runWireFixtures` (Scala 3) → "all 25 fixtures match"; `sbt + verifyGoldens` (cross 2.13 + 3) → success on both; `sbt + runWireFixtures` (cross 2.13 + 3) → "all 25 fixtures match" on both. T4 smoke test verified: corrupting a fixture (swapped key order in `Point/basic.json`) produces `RoundtripDivergence` failure naming the file; restored via seeder re-run; clean afterward. Three review rounds: T1 (scaffold) clean; T2 (harness logic + dispatch) raised 7 nit/minor (D01-D04, D06 fixed; D05 false positive; D07 deferred cosmetic); T3 (fixture authoring) clean.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **Fixture path encoding decision (orchestrator pre-pick): dots-in-dirname.** `wire-fixtures/scala/<wireId>/<scenario>.json` where `<wireId>` is a single directory name with dots (e.g. `idltest.events.TestBuzzer.EmptyInput`). Loader: `wireId = parent.name`, `scenario = file.fileName.stripSuffix(".json")`. Locked in `docs/drafts/20260504-1100-PR0302-layer-b-scala-plan.md` §3 + §11.
  - **Plan vs reality drift on `Encoder[Option].None`** (plan §6 / §11 said "Circe omits the key"; reality: `deriveEncoder` for case-class with `Option` field emits `"key":null`, NOT key omission). Empirically verified: `OptionalObj/with-none.json = {"no":null}` round-trips byte-identically. The legacy emitter does NOT use `dropNullValues`. Plan was wrong; fixture is correct.
  - **Interface elements wrap under discriminator key**: `Set[AllTypes]` and `List[AllTypes]` in `AllTypes.Struct` encode each element as `{"izumi.test.domain01.AllTypes.Struct": {…}}`. The interface-level encoder always emits the wireId-keyed wrapper; this is the master plan §4 sealed-trait encoding rule.
  - **`uint8`/`uint16`/`uint32` Scala representation is signed**: legacy Scala emitter maps these to `Byte`/`Short`/`Int`, so `200.toByte = -56`, `60000.toShort = -5536`, `4000000000L.toInt = -294967296`. The fixtures capture these signed-wrap values; semantically the bytes-on-the-wire are correct because the decoder applies the inverse wrap.
  - **ADT branch typed values must be cast to the sealed trait** before `.asJson` — branch case classes have no `Encoder` for their singleton type. Seeder uses explicit `: AdtTester` / `: AdtWithInterface` / `: JSONLike` / `: DepartmentEnum` annotations.
  - **Interface vs concrete-Struct deviations from plan §5**: the plan listed wireIds like `idltest.inheritance.Empty`, `idltest.inheritance.WithCovariance`, `izumi.test.domain01.AllTypes` (interfaces). Empirically the legacy compiler emits the sealed-trait discriminated encoder on the interface companion and the plain `deriveEncoder` on a nested `Struct` concrete case class. Fixtures dispatch to the concrete `<TypeName>.Struct` (e.g. `Empty.Struct`, `WithCovariance.Struct`, `AllTypes.Struct`), exercising the plain encoder. Coverage of the discriminator path comes from `AdtTester` and `AdtWithInterface` ADT fixtures.
  - **Service / Buzzer naming**: plan §5 row 16-17 named `GreeterService.greet.Input` / `hello.Output` speculatively; actual goldens have `TestService.SimpleInput` and `TestService.GreetSingularOutOutput`. Buzzer rows 18-20 use `TestBuzzer.{EmptyInput, EnumInputInput, AdtInputInput}` (note doubled "Input" suffix on `EnumInputInput` — that matches the actual generated class name).
  - **`FixtureSeeder` retained as permanent harness diagnostic** under `harness/FixtureSeeder.scala`. Invoked via `sbt 'runMain izumi.idealingua.harness.FixtureSeederMain <repoRoot>'`. Keeps the typed-value-construction documented in source rather than only in the JSON output. Determinism verified — no `System.currentTimeMillis`, `Random`, or environment reads.
  - **22 distinct WireDispatch entries vs ~30 fixtures** — plan §5 listed 30 rows but several were "covered by" same-type-multiple-scenarios (Point ×2, OptionalObj ×2, AdtTester ×2). Final ratio: 22 wireIds, 25 fixtures. Single-element TSet only (multi-element deferred to F8/PR-03.4).
  - **Cross-build empirically symmetric**: byte-identical fixture round-trip on both Scala 2.13.18 and 3.8.3. No Circe-derivation asymmetry observed. Plan §11 R2 risk did not materialize.
  - **`unmanagedSourceDirectories`-promotion of `golden/scala/`** adds 214 sources to the harness's compile path. Compile time ~60-120s on cold start; incremental compile is fast. PR-03.1 contracts (`verifyGoldens`, `regenerateGoldens`) unchanged.

- **PR-03.1** (2026-05-04) — Pre-modernization Layer A goldens harness landed. New sbt module `idealingua-v1-test-harness` cross-built Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3, four contractual sbt tasks (`regenerateGoldens`, `verifyGoldens`, `runWireFixtures`, `runCrossLangInterop`) wired via `(Compile / runner).value.run(mainClass, fullClasspath, args, log)` against `RegenerateMain` / `VerifyMain` entry points (the harness module stays sbt-library-free; sbt logging happens in the task body). 700 Layer A goldens generated and checked in under `idealingua-v1-test-defs/golden/{scala,typescript,csharp}/` (214 scala, 257 typescript, 229 csharp) covering all 28 `.domain` files under `main-tests/source/` (22 in `idltest/`, 5 in `izumi/test/{clashing.domain, clashing/another.domain, domain01, domain02, domain03recursive01}`, 1 in `overlaytest/withoverlay.domain`). Cross-JVM determinism verified empirically: 700-file sha256 set matches across two cold `sbt -batch regenerateGoldens` invocations. Smoke test verified: corrupting one byte of a golden produces `[error] (idealingua-v1-test-harness / verifyGoldens) … Golden mismatch: scala/idltest/aliases/package-object.scala`; sbt daemon stays live; `sbt regenerateGoldens` restores cleanly. Verification: `sbt -batch idealingua-v1-test-harness/compile` (Scala 3) → success; `sbt -batch '++ 2.13.18 idealingua-v1-test-harness/compile'` (Scala 2.13) → success; `sbt -batch verifyGoldens` → success. Three review rounds; round 1 (T1 scaffold) raised 8 nit/minor (D01-D08), 2 fixed (D01 message-text drift, D05 `private[harness]` modifier) + 6 deferred or accepted; round 2 (T2/T3 wiring) surfaced PR-03.1-D09 (legacy `ScalaTranslator` HashMap-iteration non-determinism — F6) plus D10 (`runner.value.run` architecture decision); round 3 (final pass on full diff) raised PR-03.1-D11 (`VerifyMain.main` `sys.exit(1)` killing sbt JVM, sbt 1.12 dropped TrapExit) + D12 (delete-during-iteration in `Files.list`) + D13 (residual `groupBy` non-determinism in TS/C# transpilers, deferred to F7); both D11 and D12 fixed.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **Build entrypoint is `sbtgen/Deps.scala`, not `build.sbt`** — `build.sbt` is autogenerated. Regen via `./sbtgen.sc --js`. The `--js` flag is critical — without it, sbtgen drops the four Scala.js plugin declarations from `project/plugins.sbt` and JS modules from `build.sbt`. The `sbtgen.sc` wrapper passes `"$@"` so the flag must be supplied at every regen invocation. Add to project conventions: any sbtgen regen on this repo is `./sbtgen.sc --js` from now on.
  - **F6: legacy `ScalaTranslator` is non-deterministic** at `idealingua-v1-transpilers/.../toscala/ScalaTranslator.scala:33-38`. `.toMultimap.view.mapValues(_.flatten.toSeq)` returns an `immutable.HashMap` whose iteration order leaks to package-object.scala alias emission. Two cold sbt-JVM `regenerateGoldens` runs produced different alias orderings on `idltest/aliases`, `idltest/aliases2`, `izumi/test/domain01..03`. Fixed in PR-03.1 with a 13-line surgical change: `.groupBy(_._1).toSeq.sortBy(_._1.toString).map { case (id, pairs) => id -> pairs.flatMap(_._2) }`. User explicitly approved the deviation from "purely additive" ("Forget 'purely additive' — that's acceptable"). The fix preserves declaration order *within* each ModuleId group (intra-group via `flatMap`) and sorts cross-group by `ModuleId.toString` (deterministic across JVMs).
  - **F5 / TBLOB-emitter divergence (out of PR-03.1 scope; tracked separately)**: planner audit during PR-03.1 found that ALL three transpilers diverge from the locked Q3 (base64-string for TBLOB). Scala maps `TBLOB → Array[Byte]` (Circe encodes as JSON int-array, NOT base64); TS emits `???` (`TypeScriptTypeConverter.scala:29, 60, 93, 182, 232, 282, 325`; `IntrospectionExtension.scala:34`); C# also emits `???` (PR-03-D02 in `defects-m1.md`). PR-03.1 corpus has zero TBLOB use, so F2 is vacuous (`grep -rn "blb\b\|TBLOB" …/main-tests/source/` returns no matches). Q3 must be reconfirmed before any PR-03.3 fixture authoring that includes TBLOB.
  - **F7 / cross-machine determinism residual risk (deferred)**: `TypeScriptImports.render`, `TypeScriptImports.scala:212`, `CSharpImports.scala:112`, `ServiceRenderer.scala:65`, `ScalaLayouter.scala:52` use the same `groupBy → iterate → emit-to-output` pattern as F6's pre-fix ScalaTranslator. Empirical 700-file sha256 audit on the dev host shows current goldens reproducible in two cold sbt-JVM invocations, but cross-machine determinism is not proven. If CI flakes on a different JVM/host, extend the F6-style sort fix to these sites. Recorded for forward action.
  - **Architecture: `runner.value.run(mainClass, fullClasspath, args, log)` task-body pattern.** Plan §3 sketched a direct method call from the task body, but task bodies are compiled against the meta-build classpath (in `project/`), which does NOT contain the harness module's compiled classes. Used the idiomatic sbt pattern: define `RegenerateMain` / `VerifyMain` as `def main(args: Array[String]): Unit` entry points in the harness; invoke via `r.run(mainClass, (Compile / fullClasspath).value.files, Seq(repoRoot), log)` from the task body. The `runner` does NOT fork by default — same sbt JVM, fresh classloader scope. Harness module stays sbt-library-free.
  - **`VerifyMain` failure path: never call `sys.exit`.** sbt 1.12 removed `TrapExit` (JDK 17+ deprecates `SecurityManager`); a literal `sys.exit(1)` from a task-body-invoked main kills the sbt daemon. Failures must propagate as exceptions and be converted via `r.run(...).failed.foreach(e => throw new MessageOnlyException(e.getMessage))`. Pattern recorded for any future task-body main-class invocation in this repo.
  - **C13 corpus = 28 files (broader scope)** resolved by user 2026-05-04. The narrower 22-file `idltest/` interpretation in master plan §12 PR-03.1 was superseded by §6 audit's broader assumptions (rows 17, 22 cite `phase`, `clones`, `aliases2`, `izumi/*`). §11 Q9 marked `[x]` resolved with the same answer; §12 PR-03.1 wording updated.
  - **Streams handling (per C5)**: `streams.domain` compiles cleanly through the legacy typer; the Scala/TS/C# translators silently iterate `domain.types`/`domain.services`/`domain.buzzers` only — `domain.streams` is never read. Goldens for `streams.domain` contain only the `Nuthing` DTO output (Scala: `golden/scala/idltest/streams/Nuthing.scala`; TS: `golden/typescript/idltest/streams/streams.ts`+`index.ts` minus the streams definition; C#: `golden/csharp/idltest/streams/Nuthing.cs`). Matches the locked C5 ("deprecated-but-supported, no behavior change").
  - **Goldens layout**: `idealingua-v1-test-defs/golden/{scala,typescript,csharp}/<modulePath>/<moduleName>` — outside `src/main/`, off the classpath, but colocated with the corpus they describe. Note: the master plan §3 line 191's `src/main/resources/golden/` location would have put goldens on `test-defs`'s compile classpath; PR-03.1 moved them out per the orchestrator brief.
  - **Manifest determinism (R3)**: `HarnessOptions` pins `Common.example.copy(izumiVersion = "test-harness", version = ProjectVersion("0.0.0", release = true, snapshotQualifier = "test"))` and uses PLAIN layouts for all three languages. PLAIN suppresses YARN package.json emission (`TypescriptLayouter.scala:21, 39, 45, 57` gates), NUGET csproj emission, and SBT layouter macro reads. Empirical reproducibility-check confirms no `MacroParameters.*`-driven content leaks to goldens.
  - **Clashing-domain risk (R2) resolved positive**: `izumi/test/clashing.domain` and `izumi/test/clashing/another.domain` both load successfully — they declare different domain IDs, so the `DuplicateDomainsRule` does not trip. Goldens include both. No exclusion necessary.
  - **Freeze tag NOT cut in this commit**. Per L6 the locked tag string is `wire-format-baseline-2026-05-03`; cut by the user manually after merge. The date in the tag string is fixed and does not move.

- **PR-02** (2026-05-03) — Wrote `docs/drafts/20260503-PR02-idealingua-modernization-plan.md` (~1500 lines), the master plan for replacing the legacy `IDLPostTyper`/`TypespaceImpl` with a multi-phase typer + materialized IR. Twelve sections: §1 goal/scope, §2 strategy (build alongside, feature-flag in `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade`, atomic-group rules), §3 phase-by-phase typer design (Phase 0 cross-domain index → Phase 12 Validator/Assembler, each with named Input/Output IR + Replaces in current code), §4 IR pseudo-code (Domain, Member, FlatStruct, Fingerprint as `IArray[Byte]`, the field-ordering invariant), §5 backend deletion plan (Go, Protobuf, glue with file:line citations), §6 backend restructure for Scala/TS/C# (with wire-format invariants per backend), §7 CLI/packaging, §8 performance plan, §9 cross-cutting decisions C1-C12, §10 file-level diff table, §11 risks/assumptions, §12 open questions. Verification: 16 defects in round 1 (6 major, 9 minor, 1 nit) — all resolved; round 2 caught one regressed fix (D11→D17, type mismatch fixed by introducing `Domain.userTypes` projection); round 3 verified clean.
  Notes / surprises:
  - The user's "delete Go" mandate had more glue than the meta-plan listed: `Codecs.scala` had 6 implicit defs (not 2); `CredentialsReader.scala` and `CommandlineIDLCompiler.scala:283` were missed entirely. Round-1 review caught all of these; PR-02 §5/§10 now enumerates them.
  - `IArray[Byte]` not `Array[Byte]` for `Fingerprint`. Scala's `Array[Byte]` uses reference equality; the frozen-after-assembly invariant requires structural equality, so the IR uses `IArray[Byte]` (or `ByteVector` as a Scala-2 fallback). PR-02 §4 frozen-invariant section calls this out.
  - The `typespace/structures/` subtree (5 files) is unreachable after IMPL-10 — added to the §10 deletion plan.
  - `ReservedKeywordRule` is **kept**, not deleted: it is an opt-in per-translator rule (cited in each `*TranslatorDescriptor.scala:24`), distinct from the 7 universal rules in `TypespaceVerifier.basicRules`.
  - `ScalaTranslator.scala:71-93` has no `q"trait"` quasiquote; the trait quasiquote lives in `InterfaceRenderer.scala:46-50`. Citation corrected.
  - `Domain.userTypes: Map[TypeId, TypeDef]` is a projection convenience over `Domain.members: Map[TypeId, Member]`. The legacy `ctx.typespace(a)` call sites should map to `ctx.domain.userTypes(a)` so the existing `case TypeDef.Alias(_, target, _)` pattern match continues to compile.
  - Phase numbering convention: cross-domain index = Phase 0 (precondition), structural-fact phases = Phases 5-11. IMPL-N references in §2 must use these numbers.

- **PR-03** (2026-05-03) — Wrote `docs/drafts/20260503-PR03-backcompat-test-harness-plan.md` (~1100 lines), the wire-format safety-net plan. Twelve sections: §1 goal/non-goals, §2 threat model (8 numbered failure modes, each with current-code file:line), §3 three-layer harness (A: generated-source goldens with character-for-character source equality; B: wire-byte fixtures with byte-strict raw comparison as the default; C: cross-language interop tests with generated dispatch tables), §4 wire-format spec skeleton (10 numbered topics, each citing the rule's authoring site), §5 pre-modernization baseline workflow with committed freeze tag `wire-format-baseline-2026-05-03` and four contractual sbt task names (`regenerateGoldens`, `verifyGoldens`, `runWireFixtures`, `runCrossLangInterop`), §6 corpus coverage audit (27 patterns), §7 negative tests (kind-of-diagnostic assertions deferred to PR-02), §8 CI integration, §9 module layout (Option B — new `idealingua-v1-test-harness` module), §10 risks, §11 open questions (12 of them, including TUInt64-near-2^63 policy, TSet iteration order, TBLOB encoding given the C# `???` stub), §12 implementation PR breakdown (PR-03.1 through PR-03.6). Verification: round 1 found 11 defects (3 major, 5 minor, 3 nit) — all resolved; round 2 found 2 minor leftovers (Q3 contradicting §7, freeze-tag hedge undermining D04) — resolved; round 3 caught a ghost `2026-MM-DD` placeholder at line 1160 (resolved); final review clean.
  Notes / surprises:
  - **Byte-strict raw is the default for Layer B**, not canonicalization. Earlier draft demoted byte-strictness to envelope-only and applied canonicalization to inner payloads; that silently accepted field-order regressions. The corrected policy: byte-strict raw for ALL payloads; canonicalization is a *parallel* sanity check that catches cross-language drift.
  - **C# emits `???` for TBLOB** (`JsonNetExtension.scala:188`), so there is no current C# baseline for TBLOB. PR-02 cannot ship a TBLOB change because there is no fixed point. §11 Q12 captures this.
  - **TS Identifier serialization** at `TypeScriptTranslator.scala:500-508` (`toString`/`serialize`) and `:484-491` (string-form parser); TS struct serialize/deserialize at `:205-209,190-201,515-523`. Citations now precise.
  - **Layer C drivers need generated dispatch tables** (one entry per type in the corpus) because TS and C# erase types at runtime. The harness build emits `Dispatch.ts`/`Dispatch.cs` files mapping wireId → typed encode/decode call.
  - **Freeze tag `wire-format-baseline-2026-05-03`** is a committed contract — the date does not float to baseline-landing day.
  - The `idealingua-v1-test-defs/.../source/` corpus has 22 `.domain` files plus subdirs `izumi/` and `overlaytest/` (not `izumi/test/` or `overlays/` as initially miswritten).

- **PR-01** (2026-05-03) — Wrote `docs/drafts/20260503-PR01-baboon-typer-lessons.md` (810 lines), the architectural reference that PR-02 cites. Covers: top-level Baboon pipeline mapped onto idealingua-v1, per-phase coverage of `BaboonTyper.process` (~13 phases plus trivial gluing steps, source-grounded against `BaboonTyper.scala` on the `main` branch), surrounding infrastructure (`ScopeBuilder`, `ScopeSupport`, `BaboonRules`, `BaboonEnquiries`, `RootExtractor`, `AdtInheritanceExpander`, `SymbolNames`, `TypeInfo`, `BaboonValidator`, `BaboonFamilyManager`, with `BaboonComparator` explicitly DROP), IR-level table (Raw → Scoped → Resolved → Structural → Validated → Final), 10 specific transferable lessons each anchored in idealingua-v1 file:line, non-goals (version evolution, UEBA codecs, conversion derivation, version-axis renaming, `any` builtin), and 7 open questions about Baboon. Verification: round 1 review found 17 defects (2 major, 9 minor, 6 nit); a single fix subagent applied all 17 in a single Edit session; round 2 review found no new defects. Two variant fixes flagged for re-reviewer (D08 — row already existed; D13 — `RawTypeDef.Anyvals` does not exist) were verified correct in round 2.
  Notes / surprises:
  - Baboon's default branch is `main`, not `develop`; the meta-plan's incidental `develop` references are stale and should be ignored. The header note in PR-01's deliverable now records this.
  - The originally suggested 13-phase list elides (a) the alias-root post-step on `RootExtractor.roots` (`BaboonTyper.scala:53-60`) and (b) the internal multi-step structure of `runTyper` (`:389-443`, two scope-builds around `AdtInheritanceExpander` plus a final alias sweep). Both are documented in the deliverable.
  - `BasicNamingConventionsRule` is a universal verifier rule (`TypespaceVerifier.scala:12`); `ReservedKeywordRule` is a per-translator concern (each `*TranslatorDescriptor.scala:24`). PR-02 must keep this distinction — reserved-keyword checking is translator-local, not domain-global.
  - `IDLException` throws in idealingua-v1's typer at `IDLTyper.scala:58, 157, 188, 192, 268, 322, 400, 547, 552`; `IllegalArgumentException` at `:115, :195` are intentional invariant assertions and PR-02 should preserve them as such.
  - `RawTypeDef.Anyvals` does NOT exist in the raw AST. "Anyvals" in idealingua-v1 is a Scala-translator-side optimization (`AnyvalExtension.scala:18`), not a typed-AST concept. Future work that mentions Anyvals must say which side it's referring to.

- **PR-04 MCP Mb4** (2026-05-14) — MCP / HTTP4s bridge FINAL milestone: integration tests + Layer C consistency property landed (plan `docs/drafts/20260514-mcp-http4s-bridge-plan.md` §10 + §11 Mb4). **Mb4-A** (Layer B end-to-end roundtrip, the highest-priority deliverable): new `McpBridgeRoundtripSpec` (12 cases, ~290 LOC) under `idealingua-v1-runtime-rpc-http4s/src/test/` exercises the bridge code end-to-end against `HttpRoutes.run(req)` — the FIRST time bridge code executes dispatch rather than only being golden-asserted/parse-checked through Mb1..Mb3. No BlazeServer / port-binding: tests synthesize a `Request[zio.IO[Throwable,*]]`, drive routes via `OptionT(routes.run(req).value).getOrElseF(NotFound)`, and inspect the `Response` directly — same coverage, zero socket flake. Effect wiring mirrors `Http4sTransportTest.Ctx`: bifunctor `zio.IO[+E,+A]` + `cats.effect.Async[zio.IO[Throwable, *]]` via `zio.interop.catz.asyncInstance`. Fixture `GreeterServiceMcpRoutes` (~155 LOC) is a hand-authored copy of the renderer's exact output (`DomainServiceMcpRenderer`) for the hand-written `GreeterService` (which has no IDL definition under `idealingua-v1-test-defs`). Tools/list resource lives at `src/test/resources/mcp/GreeterService.mcp.json` (60 LOC; structurally identical to the M3+M4 schema renderer's output for a wrap=true Singular + wrap=true Alternative). Test surface: (a) `GET /mcp/tools/list` returns byte-equal classpath JSON + structural shape checks; (b) `POST /mcp/tools/call greet` (Singular wrap=true) — `isError:false`, `content[0].text == structuredContent.noSpaces`, value="Hi, John Smith!"; (c) `POST /mcp/tools/call alternative` (Alternative wrap=true) — `Success("value")` round-trip; (d) unknown-method-in-prefix → in-band -32601; (e) tool-not-in-prefix → out-of-band 404; (f) per-`IRT*Exception` injection via stub `IRTServerMultiplexor.FromMethods` (7 cases — `IRTMissingHandlerException`, `IRTDecodingException`, `IRTUnparseableDataException`, `IRTTypeMismatchException`, `IRTLimitReachedException`, `IRTUnathorizedRequestContextException`, `IRTGenericFailure`) — each asserts the correct JSON-RPC 2.0 code (-32601 / -32602 / -32700 / -32602 / -32000 / -32001 / -32603 respectively) and (for the auth + missing-handler arms) the literal message text. **Mb4-B** (Layer C consistency property): new `McpBridgeConsistencySpec` (1 case, ~140 LOC) under `idealingua-v1-test-harness/src/test/`. Parses every `<Service>Mcp.scala` golden via scala.meta (manual `Tree.children` walk because the `Tree.collect`/`Tree.traverse` extension methods are Scala-2.13-only on scalameta 4.16.1), extracts `(serviceName, methodName, wrapLit)` from each `call(req, argsJson, methodId_<m>, wrap = <bool>, ...)` invocation, recompiles the IDL via `HarnessCorpus.loadCorpus`, applies `OutputWrapPolicy.isWrapped` to each `RPCMethod.signature.output`, and asserts byte-equality of the parsed-golden flag vs the computed flag. Cross-checks service sets first so a missing-service error reports cleanly. Collects ALL divergences before failing (so systematic drift surfaces as one message, not whack-a-mole). Validated **33 `(service, method)` pairs across 5 services** on the existing corpus (TestService×24, OptionalService, ImportIdService, NestedAdtsService, TestAliasServ); zero drift caught — by construction (the renderer USES `OutputWrapPolicy.isWrapped` directly), but the spec catches future drift in either direction (golden refactor without renderer update, or vice versa). **Mb4-C** (regression-harness adapter): **DEFERRED** — the existing `LangAdapter` contract (`scala-cli run <workDir>` + canonicalize stdout + diff NDJSON) is a build-and-run differ that requires a per-language sample app; a schema-only adapter doesn't fit the contract without extending the `Harness.scala` CLI surface (new `:schema`/`:mcp` lang mode, new bypass for sample-app, new diff target for emitted JSON files). Scope ballooned beyond the milestone budget; documented for a follow-up. The Mb4-A integration spec (must-have, shipped) catches the same class of regression for the in-tree corpus; Mb4-C would extend that coverage cross-compiler-version. Verification matrix (FROZEN harness contracts 4/4 + new specs): Scala 2.13.18 — `verifyGoldens` ✓, `runWireFixtures` 26+20+21 ✓, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 52/52 (was 51, +1 for Mb4-B), `idealingua-v1-runtime-rpc-http4s/test` 18/18 (was 6, +12 for Mb4-A), full `compile` clean. Scala 3.8.3 — same matrix, all green. Diff stat: 3 new files (spec under http4s + spec under test-harness + fixture under http4s/fixtures) + 1 new test resource (`GreeterService.mcp.json`); ~580 LOC test+fixture; zero prod-file edits (the renderer + manifest + `OutputWrapPolicy` are unchanged from Mb3). Surprises: **(a) schema-vs-runtime asymmetry for Singular outputs surfaced for the first time by Mb4-A's greet success-path test.** The MCP `outputSchema` declares `properties.result = {type:"string", x-idealingua-unwrap:true}` (per `SchemaMethodOutput.rawDispatch` for `Singular`), but the actual wire shape `mux.invokeMethod` emits is the IRT case-class envelope `{"value":"..."}` (Circe `deriveEncoder` over `GreeterServiceMethods.greet.Output(value: String)`). After the bridge applies its `{"result": <raw>}` wrap, `structuredContent.result` is `{"value":"Hi, John Smith!"}` — an object, not a string. The `x-idealingua-unwrap:true` annotation on the inner `result` is the contract: MCP clients are expected to unwrap the IRT case-class envelope on receive. NOT a bug, but a subtle schema-vs-runtime convention that was previously unverified; the inline comment in the test documents this for future maintainers. **(b) Scala 3 `-Wnonunit-statement` flags every intermediate `assert(...)` in a scalatest body** (each returns `org.scalatest.compatible.Assertion`, not `Unit`); silenced via class-level `@nowarn("msg=unused value of type org.scalatest.compatible.Assertion")` rather than restructuring every test into single-assertion-per-test chains — the failure messages are clearer with individual assertions. The 2.13 cross-build doesn't have the same lint, so the `@nowarn` is no-op there. **(c) scala.meta `Tree.collect`/`Tree.traverse` extensions live in a `transversers` package that didn't get a Scala 3 backport at 4.16.1** — `source.collect` fails with `E008 Not Found` on Scala 3 even with `import scala.meta.*`. Mb4-B's spec works around it with a 4-line manual depth-first walk via `Tree.children`. **(d) Effect-typeclass alignment between the renderer's generated bridge and the test was non-obvious**: the bridge requires `IO2[F]: Error2[F]` (bifunctor) AND `cats.effect.Async[F[Throwable, _]]` (http4s edge). The cleanest match was the same `zio.IO + zio.interop.catz.asyncInstance` pattern `Http4sTransportTest` already wires — no new effect-cell wiring needed. End-to-end status: the MCP bridge arc (Mb1 scaffold → Mb2 dispatch → Mb3 errors → Mb4 integration tests) is COMPLETE. The bridge is now demonstrably working end-to-end: tools/list returns a real classpath resource, tools/call dispatches into a populated `IRTServerMultiplexor` for all `Output` variants exercised, error envelopes round-trip JSON-RPC 2.0 codes per the §9 mapping, and the per-method `wrap` flag is consistency-locked against `OutputWrapPolicy.isWrapped` across renderer + goldens. Path forward (out-of-scope for this milestone): the deferred Mb4-C adapter, plus exercising more `Output` variants (Void / Struct / Algebraic) end-to-end — the corpus's IDL-driven `TestService` bridge would need its full handler implementation registered, which is significantly more scaffolding than the GreeterService two-method shape.

- **PR-04 MCP Mb3** (2026-05-14) — MCP / HTTP4s bridge error envelope refinement landed (plan `docs/drafts/20260514-mcp-http4s-bridge-plan.md` §9 + §11 Mb3). `DomainServiceMcpRenderer.call`'s `sandboxExit.flatMap` block expanded from 5 arms to 9: every `IRT*Exception` class in `IRTTransportException.scala` is now mapped explicitly to a JSON-RPC 2.0 in-band envelope, with the catch-all -32603 retained as defensive fallthrough. Final mapping (after Mb3): `IRTMissingHandlerException` → -32601 `"Method not found: <svc>.<m>"` (enriched with `methodId.service.value`/`methodId.methodId.value`); `IRTUnparseableDataException` → -32700 `"Parse error: <msg>"`; `IRTTypeMismatchException` → -32602 `"Invalid arguments (type mismatch): <msg>"`; `IRTDecodingException` → -32602 `"Invalid arguments: <msg>"`; `io.circe.Error` → -32602 `"Invalid arguments: <msg>"`; `IRTLimitReachedException` → -32000 `"Rate limit exceeded: <msg>"`; `IRTUnathorizedRequestContextException` → -32001 `"Unauthorized"`; `IRTGenericFailure` → -32603 `"Internal error"`; fallthrough → -32603 `"Internal error"`. Two intentional divergences from `HttpServer.handleHttpResult`'s precedent — both per plan §9 (which puts "unauth context" in the in-band column and reserves out-of-band HTTP for transport-layer failures only): (1) unauth now in-band -32001 instead of HTTP 401, (2) rate-limit now in-band -32000 instead of HTTP 429. Inline 11-line rationale comment on the match block documents the divergence and the choice of JSON-RPC 2.0 reserved-range codes (-32700/-32601/-32602/-32603/-32000..-32099). New private helper `safeMsg(t: Throwable): String` surfaces `getMessage` only (never cause chain or stack trace, with class-name fallback when `getMessage` is null) — prevents leaking server internals (file paths, library versions, host names) into the MCP envelope. Bridge no longer needs `org.http4s.Status` (removed from imports) or `izumi.functional.bio.F` (removed; no more `F.pure` calls — all branches go through `Ok(...)`). Added imports: `IRTGenericFailure`, `IRTLimitReachedException`, `IRTTypeMismatchException`, `IRTUnparseableDataException`. All 5 service goldens under `scala-mcp/` regenerated cleanly via `regenerateGoldens`; `verifyGoldens` re-passes. Smoke compile verification: dropped the regenerated `TestServiceMcp.scala` into `idealingua-v1-runtime-rpc-http4s/src/test` and ran `Test/compile` on Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 — clean (only the pre-existing `Error2` unused-evidence warnings carried over from Mb1; no new warnings introduced). FROZEN harness contracts 4/4 green on 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 (verifyGoldens, MCPEnvelopeValidationSpec, SchemaFixtureValidationSpec, idealingua-v1-test-harness/test 51/51). Diff stat: 1 prod file edited (renderer) + 5 goldens regenerated; +294 / -48 lines total (per-file delta uniform at +57/-8 because the match block is renderer-templated then byte-replicated into each golden); ~50 prod LOC delta (40-line match block + 11-line rationale comment + 5-line `safeMsg` helper, minus the 5-line collapsed match). Surprises: (a) plan §9 explicitly diverges from `HttpServer.handleHttpResult` for unauth (in-band vs HTTP 401) — the locked plan is the authority, captured in the inline rationale comment so future readers see why the precedent is intentionally broken. (b) `IRTMissingHandlerException` is the only enriched-context case where the message uses `methodId` from the enclosing `call(...)` parameters rather than `t.getMessage`, because the exception's own message is the IRT-internal "no handler for X" rather than a user-presentable method name — using `methodId.service.value.methodId.value` gives the same `<svc>.<m>` string the user submitted in `tools/call`. (c) `IRTGenericFailure` and the wildcard arm both map to -32603 `"Internal error"`; keeping them as separate arms (instead of collapsing into the wildcard) future-proofs against new `IRT*Exception` classes — adding a new class won't silently fall into the bucket; it'll hit the wildcard and force the maintainer to decide explicitly. Path to Mb4 (per plan §11): Layer B integration tests — round-trip an emitted bridge against a stub `IRTServerMultiplexor` over real http4s `BlazeServerBuilder` (first time bridge code runs end-to-end), assert `tools/list` JSON matches the M3+M4 `*.mcp.json`, per-`Output`-variant `tools/call` round-trip with `structuredContent` schema validation, and error-case coverage now drivable end-to-end (inject each `IRT*Exception` via the stub mux, assert each in-band code/message). Estimated 2 days per plan; minor LOC, but the first piece of test infra that doesn't piggyback on the golden pipeline.

- **PR-04 MCP Mb1** (2026-05-14) — MCP / HTTP4s bridge scaffold landed (locked-plan commit `feedc74`, plan `docs/drafts/20260514-mcp-http4s-bridge-plan.md` §11 Mb1). Two new renderer files in `idealingua-v1-transpilers`: `DomainServiceMcpRenderer` (229 LOC) emits per-service `<Name>McpRoutes` object with classpath-resource tools/list, per-method `toolName_/methodId_` consts, pattern-match dispatch, sandboxExit-based MCP envelope, and Mb1 stubs for non-`Singular` outputs; `OutputWrapPolicy` (48 LOC) shared IR-level wrap predicate consumed by both schema renderer (D6 routing through this helper) and bridge renderer. Manifest flag `ScalaBuildManifest.emitMcpBridge: Boolean = false` (D13) — opt-in, default off; Circe `deriveDecoder` picks up the new field automatically. `DomainScalaTranslator.emitService` appends `<Name>Mcp.scala` + a `mcp/<Name>.mcp.json` resource Module when gated; resource Modules carry `meta("resource") = "true"` so `ScalaLayouter` (SBT layout) routes them to `src/main/resources/<...>` instead of `src/main/scala/<...>`. Effect surface in generated code: `F[+_, +_]: IO2: Error2` plus `(implicit AT: cats.effect.Async[F[Throwable, _]])` at the edge — same split `HttpServer.scala:41` uses (plan §3.1 sketch missed the edge-Async; correction documented in the renderer scaladoc). Schema-side `wrapIfNonObject` now delegates to `OutputWrapPolicy.isWrapped` (R6 mitigation: same predicate end-to-end, behaviour-preserving — variant→bucket table identical: `Struct=false`, all four other variants `=true`). FROZEN harness extended with a virtual `scala-mcp/` golden target (`GoldenCompile.compileAll` runs a second Scala pass with `emitMcpBridge=true`, filters to MCP artefacts, deposits them under `goldenRoot/scala-mcp/`; `GoldenGenerator.regenerate` clears `scala-mcp/` alongside the three language subdirs). New goldens for all 5 corpus services + the `TestServiceMcp.scala` Layer A golden (212 LOC) and `TestService.mcp.json` (1004 lines, JSON pretty-printed). Test spec `McpBridgeEmissionSpec` (4 cases): (1) emitMcpBridge=false emits no bridge modules, (2) emitMcpBridge=true produces source+resource Modules with the expected shape (Singular routes via call, Struct stubs with Mb1 placeholder), (3) byte-equal golden assertion for `TestServiceMcp.scala` + `TestService.mcp.json`, (4) every emitted bridge source parses cleanly as Scala 2.13 via scala.meta. Smoke compile verification: dropped the emitted bridge into `idealingua-v1-runtime-rpc-http4s/src/test` and ran `Test/compile` — clean on Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 (warnings only — unused-evidence `Error2` and stub-only `methodId_*` constants for Mb1-not-implemented methods). FROZEN harness contracts 8/8 green on 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 (verifyGoldens, runWireFixtures 26+20+21, runCrossLangInterop 118 verified, idealingua-v1-test-harness/test 51/51 — up from 47 with the 4 new Mb1 cases). Diff stat: 6 edited + 4 new prod/test files + 10 new goldens; ~600 LOC source+test, ~1200 lines goldens. `IRTServerMultiplexor.invokeMethod` signature verbatim: `def invokeMethod(method: IRTMethodId)(context: C, parsedBody: Json)(implicit E: Error2[F]): F[Throwable, Json]` (matches plan §3.1 D5 — single method-id arg + (ctx, body) curry + implicit `Error2[F]`). Resource emission path: `Module(ModuleId(Seq("mcp"), "<Name>.mcp.json"), json, meta = Map("resource" -> "true"))` lands under `<output>/mcp/<Name>.mcp.json` for PLAIN layout, under `<pid>/src/main/resources/mcp/<Name>.mcp.json` for SBT layout via the meta-driven routing in `ScalaLayouter.asSbtModule`. Surprises: (a) `cats.effect.Async` is required at the http4s edge — plan D3 phrasing "NOT `cats.effect.Async`" referred to *not* replacing the bifunctor surface with Async, not banning Async at the edge; corrected without scope change. (b) The harness's `verifyGoldens` enumerates the entire `goldenRoot/` tree, so the new `scala-mcp/` subdir HAD to be wired into the golden pipeline — couldn't ship goldens out-of-band without staleness. The scaffold change is small (1 new const + 1 new for-loop in `GoldenCompile.compileAll`, ~25 LOC) but architecturally puts the MCP target on equal footing with the three language targets in the FROZEN contract. (c) `Singular` is the only variant with a working Mb1 dispatch arm; the corpus has 11 such methods across TestService alone — Mb2 needs to land the other 4 variant arms + the wrap logic for each (already correct in the static `wrap` flag baked at codegen time).
