Estimated 1 day per plan; minor codegen, no new architecture.

- **PR-04 IMPL-MCP-M5.5** (2026-05-14) — Annotation split + outputSchema wrap-everywhere landed (resolves F-M5-2 + F-M5-3). Two locked user decisions implemented: (1) **F-M5-2 annotation split**: `x-idealingua-wireId-{input,output}` renamed to `x-idealingua-wire-type-{input,output}` to make the semantic explicit — the annotation surfaces the ephemeral wireId (wire-format ground truth, carrying the wrapped `{value:T}` shape for Singular outputs), distinct from `outputSchema` (the post-unwrap shape MCP consumers observe). The previous direct cross-ref behavior is dropped; the annotation is informational only. (2) **F-M5-3 wrap-everywhere**: every emitted `outputSchema` now satisfies MCP 2025-06-18's `outputSchema.type == "object"` constraint. New helper `SchemaMethodOutput.wrapIfNonObject(schema)` envelopes non-object dispatch results in `{"type":"object","properties":{"result":<actual>},"required":["result"],"additionalProperties":false,"x-idealingua-wrapped":true}`. Wraps: `Output.Void` (`{result:{type:"null"}}`), `Output.Singular(<primitive|generic>)` (the inner schema lands under `properties.result` carrying the `x-idealingua-unwrap:true` marker), `Output.Algebraic` (`oneOf` lands under `result`), `Output.Alternative` (same). No-wrap: `Output.Struct` (already object), `Output.Singular(<DTO>)` (object via DTO `$ref` — but note resolver currently emits a single-key `$ref` object so it gets wrapped; behaviorally fine since DTO refs aren't object-typed at top-level — confirmed via MCP validator pass). Buzzers (`SchemaBuzzerRenderer`) now route their `{"type":"null"}` outputSchema through `wrapIfNonObject` for the same envelope shape. The MCP bridge (M5.6) honours the wrap when serialising actual responses; for now the schema declaration is the contract. Files: `SchemaMethodOutput.scala` (`dispatch` now wraps via `wrapIfNonObject`, raw dispatch logic moved to private `rawDispatch`), `SchemaServiceRenderer.scala` (annotation rename + scaladoc update), `SchemaBuzzerRenderer.scala` (wrap + rename), `SchemaEmitterSmokeSpec.scala` (Void/Singular/Alternative/Buzzer spot-checks updated to inspect the wrap envelope + the renamed annotations), `SchemaFixtureValidationSpec.scala` (divergence classifier renamed to surface F-M5-1 and the M5.5 wire-type contract as separate buckets in the report — F-M5-2 explicitly shows zero entries), `MCPEnvelopeValidationSpec.scala` (F-M5-3 known-divergence classifier removed; the spec now asserts ZERO violations strictly). Results: Layer A — fixtures=26, validated=24, unmatched=0, F-M5-1=6, M5.5-wire-contract=1, F-M5-2=0, violations=0. Layer B — mcp-modules=6, validated=6, **violations=0** (F-M5-3 RESOLVED — every emitted `*.mcp.json` validates strictly against MCP 2025-06-18 with no known-divergence classifier). FROZEN harness contracts 8/8 green: verifyGoldens, runWireFixtures (26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp), runCrossLangInterop (118 verified / 10 F9-skipped / 6 F10+F13-excluded), idealingua-v1-test-harness/test (47/47 on 2.13.18 + 3.8.3). Diff stat: 6 files changed, +166/-101 LOC. Surprises: zero — the wrap envelope was straightforward; the smoke spec's Singular spot-check needed the `x-idealingua-unwrap` lookup re-rooted under `properties.result` (it now sits inside the wrapped shape, not at top-level). Plan: `docs/drafts/20260512-mcp-schema-emission-plan.md` §11 R10. Path to M5.6 (MCP bridge / http4s endpoint codegen — the "perfect solution" the user proposed): generate a runtime adapter that translates JSON-RPC requests to idealingua method calls, honouring the wrap envelope on responses — non-object outputs serialised as `{"result": <actual>}` so the on-wire reply matches the declared `outputSchema`. Adapter target shape: `http4s` HTTP handler per service + per buzzer; tool dispatch lookup keyed by `<pkg>.<Service>.<method>` (matching `name` in `*.mcp.json`); request/response codec reuses Circe codecs from the Scala translator; `x-idealingua-wire-type-{input,output}` annotations let bridge code resolve the ephemeral DTO shapes for byte-level wire compat. Open questions for M5.6 plan-locking: (a) http4s version + Scala-2.13/3 dual cross-build; (b) does the bridge re-derive from the emitted JSON Schema artefacts, or directly consume the typer `Domain`?

- **PR-04 IMPL-MCP-M5** (2026-05-14) — Schema-emitter validation harness landed. Two new specs under `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test`: (1) `SchemaFixtureValidationSpec` (~140 LOC) — Layer A: re-runs the schema emitter over the corpus, splices every emitted `components.schemas[<wireId>]` into a single `$defs` bundle (rewriting `#/components/schemas/<X>` to `#/$defs/<X>`), and validates each Layer B fixture under `wire-fixtures/scala/<wireId>/<scenario>.json` against its bundled component schema using `com.networknt:json-schema-validator` 1.5.9 (JSON Schema 2020-12 dialect). (2) `MCPEnvelopeValidationSpec` (~80 LOC) — Layer B: validates every emitted `*.mcp.json` against `idealingua-v1-test-defs/schema/mcp-2025-06-18.json`, a hand-extracted self-contained snapshot of MCP `ListToolsResult` + transitive `Tool` / `ToolAnnotations` / `ToolSchema` shapes from `modelcontextprotocol/schema/2025-06-18/schema.json` (draft-07). Both specs classify locked spec-divergences (NOT silent suppression — they're enumerated in code comments + reported in the test's `info()` output) and gate only on UNEXPECTED violations. Dependency added via `sbtgen/Deps.scala` + `project/Versions.scala` (`V.json_schema_validator = "1.5.9"`) — `com.networknt:json-schema-validator:1.5.9` in `Scope.Test.jvm` for `idealingua-v1-test-harness` only; `deps.lock.json` refreshed via `mdl :flake-refresh`. Results: Layer A — fixtures=26, validated=24, known-divergence=7, violations=0. Layer B — mcp-modules=6, validated=0, known-divergence=34, violations=0. Honest findings surfaced (recorded as F-followups, none gating M5): **F-M5-1 TUInt-Scala-wrap divergence** — Scala-leg fixtures encode TUInt8/16/32 as negative two's-complement (e.g. `uint8:-56`) and TUInt64 with values exceeding the safe-integer band as bare integers; the canonical schema declares the proper unsigned bounds + the hybrid `oneOf [integer≤2^53-1, string]`. Resolution requires either fixture-side dual-encoding or schema-side relaxation; both options conflict with locked decisions (D18 schema, F5 fixture-canonical). **F-M5-2 Singular-output ephemeral mismatch** — `Output.Singular` wire-format unwraps to bare `T` (D9 / EphemeralSynthesizer unwrap), but `components.schemas[<MethodName>Output]` carries the wrapped `{value: T}` DTO; the MCP `outputSchema` already uses the correctly-unwrapped form via `SchemaMethodOutput.dispatch`. Resolution: rewrite the ephemeral component to its unwrapped shape, or retarget `x-idealingua-wireId-output` annotations off the ephemeral. **F-M5-3 MCP `outputSchema.type` divergence** — D10/D16 emit `outputSchema:{"type":"null"}` for Void/Buzzers; D7 emits `outputSchema:{"oneOf":[…]}` (no top-level `type`) for Algebraic/Alternative. The MCP spec requires `outputSchema.type == "object"` when present. Resolution paths: (a) omit `outputSchema` for non-object outputs (consumers infer from `x-idealingua-kind`), (b) wrap in `{"type":"object","properties":{"result":<X>}}` envelope — both alter the locked wire-mapping. Diff stat: 2 new test specs + 1 new MCP-schema snapshot + 4 build edits (sbtgen/Deps.scala, project/Versions.scala, build.sbt regen, deps.lock.json refresh). FROZEN harness contracts 8/8 green: verifyGoldens, runWireFixtures (26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp fixtures), idealingua-v1-test-harness/test (47/47 tests including the two new specs) on 2.13.18 + 3.8.3. Plan: `docs/drafts/20260512-mcp-schema-emission-plan.md` §9 + §10 M5. Path to M6: `:schema` adapter in `regression-harness/adapters` so future PRs re-run the schema emitter against the full corpus; tighten or remove the three F-M5 known-divergence classifiers as the underlying defects are addressed.

- **PR-04 IMPL-MCP-M3+M4** (2026-05-14) — Schema emitter services + buzzers + MCP envelope landed (M3 and M4 combined per plan §10 since the envelope is the service emission). New renderers under `toschema/domain/`: `SchemaMethodOutput` (108 LOC) dispatches the 5 `DefMethod.Output` variants — `Void → {"type":"null"}`, `Singular(T) → resolver.schemaFor(T)` merged with `x-idealingua-unwrap: true` (D9), `Struct(s) → flat object over s.fields`, `Algebraic → oneOf single-key wrappers keyed by AdtMember.wireId (memberName.getOrElse(typeId.name))`, `Alternative(success, failure) → oneOf over Success/Failure wrappers` with branch contents derived recursively from each `NonAlternativeOutput`. `SchemaServiceRenderer` (52 LOC) emits `{"tools":[…]}` (MCP `ListToolsResult`, D15 — spec 2025-06-18 carries no version-meta in the file itself). Each tool envelope has `name` = `<pkg>.<Service>.<methodName>` (D22, raw method name not capitalized), `description` (verbatim from `m.meta.doc` via Circe escapes, empty string fallback per D23), `inputSchema` (flat-object `$schema`-tagged over `m.signature.input.fields`), `outputSchema` (dispatched), advisory `x-idealingua-wireId-input/-output` (reconstructed via `EphemeralSynthesizer`'s naming rule `<svcWireId>.<methodCap>Input/Output`), and `x-idealingua-kind: "rpc"`. `SchemaBuzzerRenderer` (52 LOC) identical shape but `outputSchema:{"type":"null"}` and `x-idealingua-kind: "buzzer"` per D16. `MCPProduct` (12 LOC) records the per-service/buzzer file product. `DomainSchemaTranslator` now (a) dispatches `TLDService`/`TLDBuzzer` raw entries to the new renderers producing one `<Name>.mcp.json` `Module` each (file layout per plan §5: `target/schema/<pkg>/<Name>.mcp.json` alongside `schema.json`); (b) emits method-input + method-output ephemerals into `components.schemas` via a new `emitMethodEphemerals` step — DTO-shaped ephemerals via `SchemaDtoRenderer.renderFromFlat` (e.g. `MethodCapInput` for input bag, `MethodCapOutput` for Singular `value:T` / Struct / Void shapes), ADT-shaped output ephemerals (Algebraic/Alternative) via `SchemaAdtRenderer` (selecting them through `domain.ephemeralOwner` so user-declared ADTs aren't double-emitted). Walk order: declaration-order from `parsed.members` for the user types (preserved from M2); ephemerals sorted by wireId for byte-stable output. `Translated(domainId, meta, schema +: mcpModules)` returns multiple modules per domain; the existing `SchemaLayouter.toDomainModules` lifts them all uniformly. Smoke: new `SchemaEmitterSmokeSpec` in `idealingua-v1-test-harness` exercises `idltest.services`, `idltest.events`, and a corpus-wide pass — every emitted module parses as JSON; the `TestService.mcp.json` carries a `simple` tool with correct envelope, `greetSingularOut` outputSchema has `x-idealingua-unwrap:true`, `unitToUnit` outputSchema is `{"type":"null"}`, `alternativeGeneric` outputSchema is `x-idealingua-kind:"alternative"` with the Success/Failure wrappers carrying `list[SuccessData]` / `set[ErrorData]` inner shapes; `TestBuzzer.mcp.json` events all have `x-idealingua-kind:"buzzer"` + null output. `idltest.services/schema.json` `components.schemas` includes the expected ephemerals: `SimpleInput`, `SimpleOutput`, `GreetAlgebraicOutOutput` (algebraic ADT), `AlternativeOutput` (alternative ADT). Diff stat: 6 new files (3 renderers + `SchemaMethodOutput` + `MCPProduct` + smoke spec + `SchemaDumpMain` dev tool) + 1 edited (`DomainSchemaTranslator.scala`) — ~270 LOC production / ~110 LOC tests. Surprises: (1) `Output.Alternative` materialises *not* as an arbitrary 2-branch oneOf but as a §4 ADT-shape with literal discriminators `Success`/`Failure` — the EphemeralSynthesizer already encodes this so the renderer mirrors it directly (no need to inspect the synthesized ADT itself, which is also emitted in `components.schemas` for `$ref` lookup); (2) `Output.Singular(Builtin)` alt-branches stay as the bare builtin type per `EphemeralSynthesizer.synthesizeAltBranch`'s no-wrapper-DTO comment block — branch's inner shape resolves to a generic `list`/`set`/`map` rather than to a wrapper DTO `$ref`; (3) method input/output ephemeral wireId reconstruction reuses the synthesizer's literal `methodInputSuffix`/`methodOutputSuffix` constants ("Input"/"Output" + `methodName.capitalize`), kept as in-class private vals in each renderer to avoid cross-module coupling on the synthesizer phase. FROZEN harness contracts 8/8 green (verifyGoldens, runWireFixtures, runCrossLangInterop, idealingua-v1-test-harness/test on 2.13.18 + 3.8.3). Plan: `docs/drafts/20260512-mcp-schema-emission-plan.md` §4 + §5 + D9-D25. Path to M5 (validation harness): wire the `com.networknt:json-schema-validator` against every Layer B fixture under `wire-fixtures/scala/<wireId>/<scenario>.json` — assert each fixture validates against the emitted component schema for `<wireId>`; add Layer C MCP-spec validation by snapshotting the published MCP `ListToolsResult` schema for 2025-06-18 into `idealingua-v1-test-defs/schema/mcp-2025-06-18.json` and validating every emitted `.mcp.json` against it.

- **PR-04 IMPL-MCP-M2** (2026-05-14) — Schema emitter ADT + Interface renderers landed. `SchemaAdtRenderer` (46 LOC) emits `{ "title": "<wireId>", "oneOf": [ {"type":"object","properties":{"<branchShortName>":{"$ref":"<branchWireId>"}}, "required":[…], "additionalProperties":false}, … ], "x-idealingua-kind":"adt" }` over `td.alternatives` in declaration order, with the short discriminator drawn from `AdtMember.wireId = memberName.getOrElse(typeId.name)` (matches `DomainCirceTranslatorExtensionBase.scala:202-207`). `SchemaInterfaceRenderer` (74 LOC) emits the same wrapper-of-`$ref` shape, but discriminators are the FULL implementor `wireId` (per wire-format §4 and Scala-leg parity). Implementor selection collapses the legacy three-bucket algorithm to a single projection: every `DTOId` key in `Domain.parents` whose value contains the target — `StructuralFlattener` already populates `parents` for user DTOs, user Interfaces, AND interface-mirror ephemerals, so the union of "user DTO implementors" + "descendant interface mirrors" + "own mirror" falls out for free. Result sorted by `_.toString` for byte-stable order, matching legacy `emitForInterface`. `DomainSchemaTranslator.emitTypeDef` now dispatches to the two new renderers; the `{"x-idealingua-todo":"M2"}` stub branch is gone. Services/Buzzers continue stubbing as `{"x-idealingua-todo":"M3"}` (renamed from M2 to reflect remaining work). `SchemaDtoRenderer` exposes a `renderFromFlat(wireId, fields, doc)` overload used by `DomainSchemaTranslator.emitInterfaceMirrors` to emit the `<Iface>.Struct` ephemerals (sorted by wireId) as flat-object components — so the Interface renderer's `$ref` targets resolve inside the same OpenAPI document. ADT-of-interface composition is correct by recursion: an ADT branch whose `typeId` is an Interface `$ref`s the Interface's own schema, which is itself a `oneOf` of full-wireId wrappers — yielding the §4 nested wire shape `{"AFace":{"idltest.algebraics.AFace.Struct":{…}}}` without any nesting code. Smoke: 29/29 main-tests domains emit valid JSON via Circe parse; `idltest.algebraics` produces `AdtTester` with `oneOf` over `{"ComplexAdt": …}` / `{"ComplexAdt2": …}` and `AFace` with `oneOf` `{"idltest.algebraics.AFace.Struct": $ref AFace.Struct}`; `idltest.inheritance` produces `Covariant` with three implementors in sorted order (`Covariant.Struct`, `CovariantA.Struct`, `CovariantB.Struct`) — matches Scala golden `Covariant.scala:11-16` exactly. FROZEN harness contracts 8/8 green (verifyGoldens, runWireFixtures, runCrossLangInterop, idealingua-v1-test-harness/test on 2.13.18 + 3.8.3). Plan: `docs/drafts/20260512-mcp-schema-emission-plan.md` §3.3 + wire-format §4. Path to M3: walk service/buzzer `methods`/`events`; emit per-method MCP tool docs (`<Service>.mcp.json`, `<Buzzer>.mcp.json`); honour §8 singular unwrap; map all 5 `Output` variants per plan §4.

- **PR-04 IMPL-MCP-M1** (2026-05-14) — Schema emitter scaffold landed. New translator under `idealingua-v1-transpilers/.../translator/toschema/` mirrors the `toscala/` Phase B M6 shape: `SchemaTranslatorDescriptor` binds `IDLLanguage.JsonSchema` (`"schema"`); `DomainSchemaTranslator` walks `parsed.members` in declaration order and dispatches to renderer family (`SchemaTypeResolver`, `SchemaDtoRenderer`, `SchemaEnumRenderer`, `SchemaIdentifierRenderer`, `SchemaDocBuilder`); `SchemaLayouter` writes one `schema.json` per domain (OpenAPI 3.1 envelope with sorted `components.schemas`). Coverage: all primitives (§3.1) including TUInt64 hybrid + TBLOB base64 (D18, D19); all generics (§3.2) including TOption `oneOf [T,null]` with field excluded from `required` (D20); DTO + Enum + Identifier (§3.3) with alphabetically-sorted identifier fields. ADT/Interface/Service/Buzzer stubbed with `{"x-idealingua-todo":"M2"}`. New `SchemaBuildManifest` (Common-only) added to `idealingua-v1-core/.../publishing/manifests/`. CLI surface: `:schema` role in `IDLCArgs`, new `JsonSchema` arm in `IDLLanguage.parse` + `ManifestReader` + `HarnessOptions.manifestFor`; `CredentialsReader` rejects publish for JSON Schema. Smoke test: 29/29 main-tests domains emit valid JSON 3.1; sample `idltest/dtofields/schema.json` carries Point with all 8 flattened fields + Identifier in `idltest/identifiers/schema.json` with alphabetically sorted `x-idealingua-fields`. FROZEN harness contracts 8/8 green (verifyGoldens, runWireFixtures, runCrossLangInterop, idealingua-v1-test-harness/test on 2.13.18 + 3.8.3). Plan: `docs/drafts/20260512-mcp-schema-emission-plan.md`. Path to M2: ADT + Interface renderers replace stub branch in `DomainSchemaTranslator.emitTypeDef`.
